\"\").split(\".\").filter(Boolean);\n    }\n\n    function readValueAtPath(source, path) {\n      const parts = pathParts(path);\n      if (!parts.length) return source;\n      let value = source;\n      for (const part of parts) {\n        if (value === null || value === undefined || typeof value !== \"object\" || !(part in value)) return undefined;\n        value = value[part];\n      }\n      return value;\n    }\n\n    function setValueAtPath(target, path, value) {\n      const parts = pathParts(path);\n      if (!parts.length) return;\n      let cursor = target;\n      for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i += 1) {\n        const part = parts[i];\n        if (!cursor[part] || typeof cursor[part] !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(cursor[part])) cursor[part] = {};\n        cursor = cursor[part];\n      }\n      cursor[parts[parts.length - 1]] = value;\n    }\n\n    function deleteValueAtPath(target, path) {\n      const parts = pathParts(path);\n      if (!parts.length) return false;\n      const stack = [];\n      let cursor = target;\n      for (const part of parts.slice(0, -1)) {\n        if (!cursor || typeof cursor !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(cursor) || !(part in cursor)) return false;\n        stack.push([cursor, part]);\n        cursor = cursor[part];\n      }\n      const last = parts[parts.length - 1];\n      if (!cursor || typeof cursor !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(cursor) || !(last in cursor)) return false;\n      delete cursor[last];\n      for (let i = stack.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {\n        const [parent, key] = stack[i];\n        const value = parent[key];\n        if (!value || typeof value !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(value) || Object.keys(value).length) break;\n        delete parent[key];\n      }\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function declaredStateDataPaths(state) {\n      const paths = new Set();\n      const visit = (value, prefix) => {\n        if (!value || typeof value !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(value)) return;\n        for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {\n          const path = prefix ? prefix + \".\" + key : key;\n          paths.add(path);\n          visit(child, path);\n        }\n      };\n      visit(normalizeStateDataObject(state?.data), \"\");\n      return paths;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeContextAfterModelUpdate(previousModel, nextModel, currentContext) {\n      const previousStates = new Map((previousModel?.states || []).map(state => [String(state.id || \"\"), state]));\n      const nextStates = new Map((nextModel?.states || []).map(state => [String(state.id || \"\"), state]));\n      for (const [stateId, previousState] of previousStates) {\n        if (!stateId) continue;\n        const scope = \"states.\" + stateId;\n        const nextState = nextStates.get(stateId);\n        if (!nextState) {\n          deleteValueAtPath(currentContext, scope);\n          continue;\n        }\n        const nextPaths = declaredStateDataPaths(nextState);\n        const removedPaths = [...declaredStateDataPaths(previousState)]\n          .filter(path => !nextPaths.has(path))\n          .sort((a, b) => a.split(\".\").length - b.split(\".\").length);\n        for (const path of removedPaths) deleteValueAtPath(currentContext, scope + \".\" + path);\n      }\n      if (!currentContext.state || typeof currentContext.state !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(currentContext.state)) currentContext.state = {};\n      if (currentContext.state.current === undefined) currentContext.state.current = nextModel?.initial || \"\";\n      if (currentContext.state.previous === undefined) currentContext.state.previous = \"\";\n      if (currentContext.state.lastTransition === undefined) currentContext.state.lastTransition = \"\";\n      if (!currentContext.runtime || typeof currentContext.runtime !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(currentContext.runtime)) currentContext.runtime = {};\n      if (currentContext.runtime.paused === undefined) currentContext.runtime.paused = false;\n      if (!currentContext.realtime || typeof currentContext.realtime !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(currentContext.realtime)) currentContext.realtime = {};\n      const realtimeDefaults = {\n        roomId: \"\",\n        clientId: \"\",\n        status: \"idle\",\n        connected: false,\n        joined: false,\n        connecting: false,\n        reconnectAttempt: 0,\n        error: \"\"\n      };\n      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(realtimeDefaults)) {\n        if (currentContext.realtime[key] === undefined) currentContext.realtime[key] = value;\n      }\n      return currentContext;\n    }\n\n    function resetRuntimeContextInPlace(nextModel, currentContext) {\n      for (const key of Object.keys(currentContext)) delete currentContext[key];\n      currentContext.state = {\n        current: nextModel?.initial || \"\",\n        previous: \"\",\n        lastTransition: \"\"\n      };\n      currentContext.runtime = defaultRuntimeState();\n      currentContext.realtime = {\n        roomId: \"\",\n        clientId: \"\",\n        status: \"idle\",\n        connected: false,\n        joined: false,\n        connecting: false,\n        reconnectAttempt: 0,\n        error: \"\"\n      };\n      return currentContext;\n    }\n\n    function eventSegment(text) {\n      return String(text || \"event\").trim().toLowerCase()\n        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \".\")\n        .replace(/^\\.+|\\.+$/g, \"\") || \"event\";\n    }\n\n    function normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) {\n      return transition?.triggerType === undefined ? \"button\" : String(transition.triggerType);\n    }\n\n    function normalizeTransitionTimerMs(value) {\n      const numeric = Number(value);\n      return Math.min(300000, Math.max(100, Number.isFinite(numeric) ? Math.round(numeric) : 3000));\n    }\n\n    function normalizeTransitionEvent(value, fallback = \"\") {\n      const raw = String(value || fallback || \"\").trim();\n      if (!raw) return \"\";\n      return raw.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]+/g, \".\").replace(/^\\.+|\\.+$/g, \"\");\n    }\n\n    function defaultTransitionEvent(transition) {\n      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);\n      if (triggerType === \"button\") return \"button.\" + eventSegment(transition?.id || transition?.label || \"next\") + \".clicked\";\n      if (triggerType === \"timer\") return \"timer.\" + eventSegment(transition?.label || transition?.id || \"done\") + \".done\";\n      if (triggerType === \"auto\") return \"auto.\" + eventSegment(transition?.id || transition?.label || \"next\");\n      if (triggerType === \"flow\") return \"flow.child.entry\";\n      return \"\";\n    }\n\n    function normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent(transition) {\n      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);\n      const rawTriggerEvent = normalizeTransitionEvent(transition?.triggerEvent || \"\");\n      if (triggerType === \"change\") {\n        return /^(button|event|realtime|timer|auto|flow)\\./i.test(rawTriggerEvent) ? \"\" : rawTriggerEvent;\n      }\n      if (triggerType === \"event\") {\n        return rawTriggerEvent && !/^(button|change|realtime|fetch|timer|auto|flow)\\./i.test(rawTriggerEvent)\n          ? rawTriggerEvent\n          : \"\";\n      }\n      if (triggerType === \"realtime\") {\n        return rawTriggerEvent.startsWith(\"realtime.\") ? rawTriggerEvent : \"\";\n      }\n      if (triggerType === \"api\") {\n        return /^fetch\\.[a-z0-9_.]+\\.(success|error)$/.test(rawTriggerEvent) ? rawTriggerEvent : \"\";\n      }\n      if (triggerType === \"flow\") {\n        return rawTriggerEvent === \"flow.child.entry\" ? rawTriggerEvent : \"flow.child.entry\";\n      }\n      if ([\"button\", \"timer\", \"auto\"].includes(triggerType)) {\n        const prefix = triggerType + \".\";\n        return rawTriggerEvent.toLowerCase().startsWith(prefix)\n          ? rawTriggerEvent\n          : normalizeTransitionEvent(defaultTransitionEvent({ ...transition, triggerType }));\n      }\n      return rawTriggerEvent;\n    }\n\n    function transitionTriggerType(transition) {\n      return normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);\n    }\n    function transitionEventName(transition) {\n      const type = transitionTriggerType(transition);\n      const explicit = normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent(transition);\n      if ((type === \"change\" || type === \"event\" || type === \"realtime\" || type === \"api\" || type === \"flow\") && !explicit) return \"\";\n      return explicit || normalizeTransitionEvent(defaultTransitionEvent(transition));\n    }\n\n    const RUNTIME_TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS = new Set([\"equals\", \"gt\", \"gte\", \"lt\", \"lte\", \"between\"]);\n\n    function runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatchRange(value) {\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(value)) return null;\n      const min = Number(value.min);\n      const max = Number(value.max);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(min) || !Number.isFinite(max)) return null;\n      const minInclusive = value.minInclusive !== false;\n      const maxInclusive = value.maxInclusive !== false;\n      if (min > max) return null;\n      if (min === max && (!minInclusive || !maxInclusive)) return null;\n      return { min, minInclusive, max, maxInclusive };\n    }\n\n    function runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(value) {\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(value)) return {};\n      const field = runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(value.field || \"\", \"\");\n      const operator = String(value.operator || \"\").trim();\n      if (!field || !RUNTIME_TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS.has(operator)) return {};\n      const out = { field, operator };\n      if (operator === \"between\") {\n        const range = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatchRange(value.value);\n        if (!range) return {};\n        out.value = range;\n      } else {\n        out.value = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, \"value\") ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value.value)) : \"\";\n      }\n      return out;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(match) {\n      return !isPlainDataObject(match) || !runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(match.field || \"\", \"\") || !RUNTIME_TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS.has(String(match.operator || \"\"));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchValue(value) {\n      if (typeof value === \"number\") return Number.isFinite(value) ? JSON.stringify(value) : \"\";\n      if (typeof value === \"boolean\" || typeof value === \"string\") return JSON.stringify(value);\n      if (isPlainDataObject(value) && Number.isFinite(value.min) && Number.isFinite(value.max)) {\n        return JSON.stringify({\n          min: value.min,\n          minInclusive: value.minInclusive !== false,\n          max: value.max,\n          maxInclusive: value.maxInclusive !== false\n        });\n      }\n      return \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchKey(match) {\n      const normalized = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(match);\n      if (runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(normalized)) return \"*\";\n      const value = runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchValue(normalized.value);\n      if (!value) return \"\";\n      return normalized.field + \":\" + normalized.operator + \":\" + value;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTriggerMatchInterval(match) {\n      const normalized = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(match);\n      if (runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(normalized)) return null;\n      if (normalized.operator === \"equals\") {\n        if (typeof normalized.value !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(normalized.value)) return null;\n        return { min: normalized.value, minInclusive: true, max: normalized.value, maxInclusive: true };\n      }\n      if (normalized.operator === \"gt\" || normalized.operator === \"gte\") {\n        if (typeof normalized.value !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(normalized.value)) return null;\n        return { min: normalized.value, minInclusive: normalized.operator === \"gte\", max: Infinity, maxInclusive: false };\n      }\n      if (normalized.operator === \"lt\" || normalized.operator === \"lte\") {\n        if (typeof normalized.value !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(normalized.value)) return null;\n        return { min: -Infinity, minInclusive: false, max: normalized.value, maxInclusive: normalized.operator === \"lte\" };\n      }\n      if (normalized.operator === \"between\") return runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatchRange(normalized.value);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTriggerMatchIntervalsOverlap(left, right) {\n      if (!left || !right) return true;\n      if (left.max < right.min) return false;\n      if (right.max < left.min) return false;\n      if (left.max === right.min && !(left.maxInclusive && right.minInclusive)) return false;\n      if (right.max === left.min && !(right.maxInclusive && left.minInclusive)) return false;\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionMatchesCanOverlap(left, right) {\n      const leftMatch = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(left?.triggerMatch);\n      const rightMatch = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(right?.triggerMatch);\n      if (runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(leftMatch) || runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(rightMatch)) return true;\n      if (leftMatch.field !== rightMatch.field) return true;\n      const leftInterval = runtimeTriggerMatchInterval(leftMatch);\n      const rightInterval = runtimeTriggerMatchInterval(rightMatch);\n      if (leftInterval || rightInterval) return runtimeTriggerMatchIntervalsOverlap(leftInterval, rightInterval);\n      return runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchValue(leftMatch.value) === runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchValue(rightMatch.value);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionTriggerBaseKey(transition) {\n      const type = transitionTriggerType(transition);\n      const eventName = transitionEventName(transition);\n      if (type === \"flow\") return \"\";\n      if (type === \"button\") return \"button:\" + transition.id;\n      if (type === \"auto\" || type === \"timer\") return type;\n      if (type === \"change\") return eventName ? \"change:\" + eventName : \"\";\n      return eventName ? type + \":\" + eventName : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionTriggerContractKey(transition) {\n      const baseKey = runtimeTransitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);\n      if (!baseKey) return \"\";\n      const type = transitionTriggerType(transition);\n      if (![\"change\", \"event\", \"realtime\", \"api\"].includes(type)) return baseKey;\n      const matchKey = runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition.triggerMatch);\n      return matchKey ? baseKey + \"|match:\" + matchKey : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeReadDetailValue(detail, sourcePath) {\n      const clean = runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(sourcePath || \"\", \"\");\n      if (!clean) return undefined;\n      const path = clean.startsWith(\"detail.\") ? clean.slice(\"detail.\".length) : clean;\n      return readValueAtPath(isPlainDataObject(detail) ? detail : {}, path);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeMatchValue(actual, operator, expected) {\n      if (actual === null || actual === undefined) return false;\n      if (operator === \"between\") {\n        const range = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatchRange(expected);\n        if (!range || typeof actual !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(actual)) return false;\n        if (actual < range.min || actual > range.max) return false;\n        if (actual === range.min && !range.minInclusive) return false;\n        if (actual === range.max && !range.maxInclusive) return false;\n        return true;\n      }\n      if (typeof expected === \"number\") {\n        if (typeof actual !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(actual)) return false;\n        if (operator === \"equals\") return actual === expected;\n        if (operator === \"gt\") return actual > expected;\n        if (operator === \"gte\") return actual >= expected;\n        if (operator === \"lt\") return actual < expected;\n        if (operator === \"lte\") return actual <= expected;\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (operator !== \"equals\") return false;\n      if (typeof expected === \"boolean\") return typeof actual === \"boolean\" && actual === expected;\n      if (typeof expected === \"string\") return String(actual) === expected;\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionMatchOk(transition, detail = {}) {\n      const match = runtimeNormalizeTriggerMatch(transition?.triggerMatch);\n      if (runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(match)) return true;\n      return runtimeMatchValue(runtimeReadDetailValue(detail, match.field), match.operator, match.value);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeConditionPathIsReadable(path) {\n      return /^states\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*\\./.test(path)\n        || path === \"state.current\"\n        || path === \"runtime.paused\"\n        || /^events\\./.test(path)\n        || /^emitters\\./.test(path)\n        || /^realtime\\./.test(path);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeConditionContractIssue(condition) {\n      const text = String(condition || \"\").trim();\n      if (!text) return \"\";\n      const pathPattern = \"[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\\\\.(?:[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*|\\\\d+))*\";\n      const pathOnly = new RegExp(\"^\" + pathPattern + \"$\");\n      const comparison = new RegExp(\"^(\" + pathPattern + \")\\\\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\\\\s*(.+)$\");\n      const literal = /^(?:true|false|-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?|\"(?:\\\\.|[^\"\\\\])*\"|'(?:\\\\.|[^'\\\\])*')$/;\n      for (const orPart of text.split(\"||\")) {\n        if (!orPart.trim()) return \"invalid-condition\";\n        for (let atom of orPart.split(\"&&\")) {\n          atom = atom.trim();\n          if (!atom) return \"invalid-condition\";\n          if (atom === \"true\" || atom === \"false\") continue;\n          if (atom.startsWith(\"!\")) {\n            const conditionPath = atom.slice(1).trim();\n            if (!pathOnly.test(conditionPath) || !runtimeConditionPathIsReadable(conditionPath)) return \"invalid-condition\";\n            continue;\n          }\n          const match = atom.match(comparison);\n          if (match) {\n            if (!runtimeConditionPathIsReadable(match[1]) || !literal.test(match[3].trim())) return \"invalid-condition\";\n            if ([\">\", \">=\", \"<\", \"<=\"].includes(match[2]) && !/^-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?$/.test(match[3].trim())) return \"invalid-condition\";\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (!pathOnly.test(atom) || !runtimeConditionPathIsReadable(atom)) return \"invalid-condition\";\n        }\n      }\n      return \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionTriggerContract(transitions) {\n      const all = Array.isArray(transitions) ? transitions : [];\n      const contractualTypes = new Set([\"button\", \"change\", \"event\", \"realtime\", \"api\", \"timer\", \"auto\", \"flow\"]);\n      const invalidType = all.find(transition => !contractualTypes.has(transitionTriggerType(transition)));\n      if (invalidType) return { ok: false, reason: \"invalid-trigger-type\", transitionIds: [invalidType.id] };\n      const outgoing = all.filter(transition => transitionTriggerType(transition) !== \"flow\");\n      if (outgoing.some(transition => transitionTriggerType(transition) === \"auto\") && outgoing.length !== 1) {\n        return { ok: false, reason: \"exclusive-auto\", transitionIds: outgoing.map(transition => transition.id) };\n      }\n      const claims = new Map();\n      const eventClaims = new Map();\n      for (const transition of outgoing) {\n        const conditionIssue = runtimeConditionContractIssue(transition.condition);\n        if (conditionIssue) return { ok: false, reason: conditionIssue, transitionIds: [transition.id] };\n        const rawEventName = String(transition.triggerEvent || \"\");\n        if (rawEventName && normalizeTransitionEvent(rawEventName) !== rawEventName) {\n          return { ok: false, reason: \"invalid-event-name\", transitionIds: [transition.id] };\n        }\n        const type = transitionTriggerType(transition);\n        const key = runtimeTransitionTriggerContractKey(transition);\n        const baseKey = runtimeTransitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);\n        const matchKey = runtimeCanonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition.triggerMatch);\n        if (!key) return { ok: false, reason: \"missing-trigger\", transitionIds: [transition.id] };\n        if (!runtimeTriggerMatchIsEmpty(transition.triggerMatch) && type !== \"realtime\") {\n          return { ok: false, reason: \"invalid-trigger-match\", transitionIds: [transition.id] };\n        }\n        if (claims.has(key)) return { ok: false, reason: \"duplicate-trigger\", triggerKey: key, transitionIds: [claims.get(key), transition.id] };\n        claims.set(key, transition.id);\n        if (baseKey && [\"change\", \"event\", \"realtime\", \"api\"].includes(type)) {\n          const existing = eventClaims.get(baseKey) || [];\n          const hasCatchAll = existing.some(item => item.matchKey === \"*\");\n          if ((matchKey === \"*\" && existing.length) || (matchKey !== \"*\" && hasCatchAll)) {\n            return { ok: false, reason: \"ambiguous-trigger-match\", triggerKey: baseKey, transitionIds: [...existing.map(item => item.id), transition.id] };\n          }\n          const overlapping = matchKey !== \"*\" ? existing.find(item => runtimeTransitionMatchesCanOverlap(item.transition, transition)) : null;\n          if (overlapping) {\n            return { ok: false, reason: \"ambiguous-trigger-match\", triggerKey: baseKey, transitionIds: [overlapping.id, transition.id] };\n          }\n          existing.push({ id: transition.id, matchKey, transition });\n          eventClaims.set(baseKey, existing);\n        }\n      }\n      return { ok: true, reason: \"\", transitionIds: [] };\n    }\n\n    const RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN = Symbol(\"runtime-write\");\n    const RUNTIME_AUTHORIZED_WRITE_SOURCES = new Set([\n      \"button\", \"daisy\", \"daisy-countdown\", \"event\", \"fetch\", \"flow\", \"input\",\n      \"input-default\", \"model-sync\", \"state-default\", \"transition\", \"runtime\",\n      \"runtime-empty\", \"runtime-control\", \"reset\", \"host\", \"storage\", \"initial\",\n      \"realtime\", \"runtime-replay\"\n    ]);\n\n    function runtimeWriteSource(opts = {}) {\n      return String(opts.source || \"runtime\");\n    }\n\n    function runtimeWriteSourceIsAuthorized(opts = {}) {\n      return RUNTIME_AUTHORIZED_WRITE_SOURCES.has(runtimeWriteSource(opts));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeStateDataPathIsDeclared(path) {\n      const targetPath = normalizeContextPath(path, \"\");\n      if (!targetPath) return false;\n      for (const state of Array.isArray(model?.states) ? model.states : []) {\n        const scope = \"states.\" + state.id;\n        if (!targetPath.startsWith(scope + \".\")) continue;\n        const parts = targetPath.slice(scope.length + 1).split(\".\").filter(Boolean);\n        let cursor = normalizeStateDataObject(state.data);\n        let declared = parts.length > 0;\n        for (const part of parts) {\n          if (Array.isArray(cursor) && /^\\d+$/.test(part) && Number(part) < cursor.length) {\n            cursor = cursor[Number(part)];\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (!isPlainDataObject(cursor) || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cursor, part)) {\n            declared = false;\n            break;\n          }\n          cursor = cursor[part];\n        }\n        if (declared) return true;\n      }\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeExternalWritePathIsAuthorized(path) {\n      return runtimeStateDataPathIsDeclared(path);\n    }\n\n    let runtimeTrustedUiEventActive = false;\n    let runtimeTrustedUiEventClearId = 0;\n\n    function runtimeUiEventIsTrusted(evt) {\n      return Boolean(evt && evt.isTrusted === true);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeActivateTrustedUiEvent(evt) {\n      if (!runtimeUiEventIsTrusted(evt)) return false;\n      runtimeTrustedUiEventActive = true;\n      runtimeTrustedUiEventClearId += 1;\n      const clearId = runtimeTrustedUiEventClearId;\n      const clear = () => {\n        if (runtimeTrustedUiEventClearId === clearId) runtimeTrustedUiEventActive = false;\n      };\n      setTimeout(clear, 0);\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeUiCommitIsTrusted() {\n      return runtimeTrustedUiEventActive;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeWriteSourceRequiresTrustedUiEvent(source) {\n      return source === \"button\" || source === \"daisy\" || source === \"input\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeEventDetailRequiresTrustedUiEvent(detail = {}) {\n      if (detail?.__realtimeRemote === true || detail?.source === \"realtime\") return false;\n      const source = String(detail?.source || \"\");\n      if (runtimeWriteSourceRequiresTrustedUiEvent(source)) return true;\n      return String(detail?.type || \"\") === \"button\";\n    }\n\n    [\"click\", \"input\", \"change\", \"toggle\", \"submit\", \"keydown\"].forEach(type => {\n      document.addEventListener(type, runtimeActivateTrustedUiEvent, { capture: true, passive: true });\n    });\n\n    function writeRuntimeState(path, value, opts = {}) {\n      const targetPath = normalizeContextPath(path, \"\");\n      if (!targetPath) return { path: \"\", changed: false, previous: undefined, value };\n      if (opts.token !== RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN) {\n        throw new Error(\"Unauthorized runtime bus write: \" + targetPath);\n      }\n      if (!runtimeWriteSourceIsAuthorized(opts)) {\n        throw new Error(\"Unauthorized runtime bus write source: \" + runtimeWriteSource(opts));\n      }\n      if (!runtimeValueIsDefined(value)) {\n        throw new Error(\"Runtime bus values must be fully defined: \" + targetPath);\n      }\n      const previous = readValueAtPath(context, targetPath);\n      const changed = JSON.stringify(previous) !== JSON.stringify(value);\n      if (!changed) return { path: targetPath, changed: false, previous, value };\n      setValueAtPath(context, targetPath, value);\n      if (opts.metadata !== false) {\n        setValueAtPath(context, \"lastChangedPath\", targetPath);\n        setValueAtPath(context, \"lastChangedAt\", Date.now());\n      }\n      return { path: targetPath, changed: true, previous, value };\n    }\n\n    function runtimeEventEmitterId(detail = {}) {\n      const id = String(detail?.emitterId || \"\").trim();\n      return /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_:-]*(?:\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_:-]*)*$/.test(id) ? id : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function normalizeRealtimeEmitterConfig(value) {\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(value)) return null;\n      const id = runtimeEventEmitterId({ emitterId: value.id });\n      const events = const root = \"emitters.\" + emitterId;\n      const countPath = root + \".count\";\n      const count = Number(readValueAtPath(context, countPath) || 0) + 1;\n      writeRuntimeState(countPath, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(detail)) : {}, writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(root + \".status\", \"received\", writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(root + \".error\", \"\", writeOpts);\n      return normalizeTransitionEvent(eventName);\n      if (!name) return \"\";\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const countPath = \"events.\" + name + \".count\";\n      const count = Number(readValueAtPath(context, countPath) || 0) + 1;\n      const writeOpts = { source: \"event\", metadata: false, token: RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN };\n      writeRuntimeState(countPath, count, writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(\"events.\" + name + \".lastAt\", now, writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(\"events.\" + name + \".detail\", detail, writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(\"lastEvent\", name, writeOpts);\n      writeRuntimeState(\"lastEventAt\", now, writeOpts);\n      recordRuntimeEmitter(name, detail, now, writeOpts, realtimeConfig);\n      return name;\n    }\n\n    function normalizeRealtimeSchemaType(value) {\n      return String(value || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n    }\n\n    function normalizeRealtimeDetailSchema(value) {\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(value)) return {};\n      const out = {};\n      for (const [path, type] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 64)) {\n        const cleanPath = runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(path, \"\");\n        if (cleanPath) out[cleanPath] = normalizeRealtimeSchemaType(type);\n      }\n      return out;\n    }\n\n    function normalizeRealtimeEventBindings(value) {\n      if = isPlainDataObject(value?.event) ? value.event : value;\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(source)) return null;\n      const name = normalizeTransitionEvent(source.name || \"\");\n      if (!name || !name.startsWith(\"realtime.\")) return null;\n      const detail = normalizeRealtimeDetailSchema(source.detail);\n      const matchFields = (Array.isArray(source.matchFields) ? source.matchFields :      if (!clean) return undefined;\n      const path = clean.startsWith(\"detail.\") ? clean.slice(\"detail.\".length) : clean;\n      return readValueAtPath(detail, path);\n    }\n\n    function applyRealtimeEventBindings(eventName, detail = {}, realtimeEventConfig = null) {\n      const event = normalizeRealtimeEventConfig(realtimeEventConfig);\n      if (event && event.name !== normalizeTransitionEvent(eventName || \"\")) return;\n      if (!event) return;\n      const sourceDetail = isPlainDataObject(detail) ? detail : {};\n      for (const binding of normalizeRealtimeStatus(value) {\n      const source = isPlainDataObject(value) ? value : {};\n      return {\n        roomId: String(source.roomId || \"\"),\n        clientId: String(source.clientId || \"\"),\n        status: String(source.status || \"idle\"),\n        connected: source.connected === true,\n        joined: source.joined === true,\n        connecting: source.connecting === true,\n        reconnectAttempt: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(source.reconnectAttempt) || 0)),\n        error: String(source.error || \"\")\n      };\n    }\n\n    function applyRealtimeStatus(value) {\n      const previous = readValueAtPath(context, \"realtime\");\n      const next = { ...normalizeRealtimeStatus(previous), ...normalizeRealtimeStatus(value) };\n      runtimeSet(\"realtime\", next, { \"https://realtime.digitalisierungsplanung.de/token\";\n    const runtimeRealtimeTransport = {\n      roomId: \"\",\n      clientId: \"\",\n      socket: null,\n      joined: false,\n      started: false,\n      connecting: false,\n      status: \"idle\",\n      error: \"\",\n      reconnectAttempt: 0,\n      reconnectTimer: 0\n    };\n\n    function sanitizeRuntimeRealtimeId(value) {\n      const text = String(value || \"\").trim();\n      return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(text) ? text : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRealtimeRoomId() {\n      const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search || \"\");\n      const fragment = new URLSearchParams(location.hash.slice(1));\n      return sanitizeRuntimeRealtimeId(query.get(\"room\") || fragment.get(\"room\") || \"\");\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRealtimeClientId() {\n      const random = window.crypto?.randomUUID?.()\n        || Date.now().toString(36) + \"-\" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);\n      return sanitizeRuntimeRealtimeId(\"runtime-\" + random);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRealtimeSnapshot(status = runtimeRealtimeTransport.status) {\n      return {\n        roomId: runtimeRealtimeTransport.roomId,\n        clientId: runtimeRealtimeTransport.clientId,\n        status: status || \"idle\",\n        connected: Boolean(runtimeRealtimeTransport.socket && runtimeRealtimeTransport.socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN),\n        joined: Boolean(runtimeRealtimeTransport.joined),\n        connecting: Boolean(runtimeRealtimeTransport.connecting),\n        reconnectAttempt: Math.max(0, Number(runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectAttempt) || 0),\n        error: runtimeRealtimeTransport.error || \"\"\n      };\n    }\n\n    function updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(status = \"\") {\n      if (status) runtimeRealtimeTransport.status = status;\n      applyRealtimeStatus(runtimeRealtimeSnapshot(runtimeRealtimeTransport.status));\n    }\n\n    async function fetchRuntimeRealtimeToken() {\n      const url = new URL(RUNTIME_REALTIME_TOKEN_URL);\n      url.searchParams.set(\"roomId\", runtimeRealtimeTransport.roomId);\n      url.searchParams.set(\"clientId\", runtimeRealtimeTransport.clientId);\n      const response = await fetch(url.toString(), { method: \"GET\", cache: \"no-store\", credentials: \"omit\" });\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(\"Realtime token failed\");\n      const payload = await response.json();\n      return String(payload.token || \"\");\n    }\n\n    function scheduleRuntimeRealtimeReconnect() {\n      if (!runtimeRealtimeTransport.started || runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectTimer) return;\n      updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"reconnecting\");\n      const delay = Math.min(10000, 500 * Math.pow(2, runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectAttempt++));\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n        runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectTimer = 0;\n        connectRuntimeRealtimeTransport();\n      }, delay);\n    }\n\n    async function connectRuntimeRealtimeTransport() {\n      if (!runtimeRealtimeTransport.started || runtimeRealtimeTransport.connecting || !window.WebSocket || !window.fetch) return;\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.connecting = true;\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.error = \"\";\n      updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"connecting\");\n      try {\n        const token = await fetchRuntimeRealtimeToken();\n        const socket = new WebSocket(RUNTIME_REALTIME_WSS_URL);\n        runtimeRealtimeTransport.socket = socket;\n        runtimeRealtimeTransport.joined = false;\n        socket.addEventListener(\"open\", () => {\n          updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"joining\");\n          socket.send(JSON.stringify({\n            type: \"join\",\n            roomId: runtimeRealtimeTransport.roomId,\n            clientId: runtimeRealtimeTransport.clientId,\n            token\n          }));\n        });\n        socket.addEventListener(\"message\", evt => {\n          let message = null;\n          try { message = JSON.parse(String(evt.data || \"\")); } catch (_) {}\n          if (!message || typeof message !== \"object\") return;\n          if (message.type === \"joined\") {\n            runtimeRealtimeTransport.joined = true;\n            runtimeRealtimeTransport.reconnectAttempt = 0;\n            runtimeRealtimeTransport.error = \"\";\n            updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"joined\");\n            return;\n          }\n          if (message.type !== \"runtime.event\" || message.clientId === runtimeRealtimeTransport.clientId) return;\n          const name = normalizeTransitionEvent(message.name || \"\");\n          const event = normalizeRealtimeEventConfig(message.event);\n          if (!name || !name.startsWith(\"realtime.\") || !event || event.name !== name) return;\n          const sourceDetail = isPlainDataObject(message.detail) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(message.detail)) : {};\n          emitRuntimeEvent(name, {\n            ...sourceDetail,\n            type: \"realtime\",\n            source: \"realtime\",\n            __realtimeRemote: true,\n            emitterId: String(message.emitterId || \"\"),\n            roomId: sanitizeRuntimeRealtimeId(message.roomId || runtimeRealtimeTransport.roomId),\n            remoteClientId: sanitizeRuntimeRealtimeId(message.clientId || \"\"),\n            serverTime: Number(message.serverTime) || Date.now()\n          }, { event, emitter: message.emitter });\n        });\n        socket.addEventListener(\"close\", () => {\n          runtimeRealtimeTransport.joined = false;\n          runtimeRealtimeTransport.socket = null;\n          updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"reconnecting\");\n          scheduleRuntimeRealtimeReconnect();\n        });\n        socket.addEventListener(\"error\", () => {\n          runtimeRealtimeTransport.error = \"socket_error\";\n          updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"error\");\n        });\n      } catch (_) {\n        runtimeRealtimeTransport.error = \"connect_failed\";\n        updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus(\"error\");\n        scheduleRuntimeRealtimeReconnect();\n      } finally {\n        runtimeRealtimeTransport.connecting = false;\n        updateRuntimeRealtimeStatus();\n      }\n    }\n\n    function startRuntimeRealtimeTransport() {\n      if (runtimeRealtimeTransport.started) return;\n      const roomId = runtimeRealtimeRoomId();\n      if (!roomId) return;\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.roomId = roomId;\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.clientId = runtimeRealtimeClientId();\n      runtimeRealtimeTransport.started = true;\n      connectRuntimeRealtimeTransport();\n    }\n\n    function pathMatchesSubscription(path, subscription) {\n      const p = normalizeContextPath(path, \"\");\n      const s = normalizeSubscriptionPath(subscription);\n      if (!p || !s) return false;\n      if (s === \"*\") return true;\n      return p === s || p.startsWith(s + \".\") || s.startsWith(p + \".\");\n    }\n\n    function stateSubscribesToPath(state, path) {\n      if (!state || !path) return false;\n      return normalizeSubscriptions(state.subscriptions).some(subscription => pathMatchesSubscription(path, subscription));\n    }\n\n    function candidateStatesForRuntimeEvent(eventName, detail = {}) {\n      const state = stateById(current);\n      if (!state) return [];\n      const runtimeEventKind = String(detail?.type || \"\");\n      if (runtimeEventKind && runtimeEventKind !== \"change\" && runtimeEventKind !== \"fetch\") return [state];\n      if stateSubscribesToPath(item, eventPath));\n      return subscribed.length ? subscribed : [state];\n    }\n\n    function transitionMatchesRuntimeEvent(transition, eventName, detail = {}) {\n      if (detail?.transitionId && transition?.id !== detail.transitionId) return false;\n      const type = transitionTriggerType(transition);\n      const configured = transitionEventName(transition);\n      if (type === \"button\" || type === \"event\" || type === \"realtime\" || type === \"api\" || type === \"timer\" || type === \"auto\" || type === \"flow\") return configured === eventName && runtimeTransitionMatchOk(transition, detail);\n      if (type === \"change\") {\n        const path = normalizeContextPath(detail.path || \"\", \"\");\n        if (!configured) return Boolean(path);\n        return configured === eventName || configured === path || configured === \"change.\" + path || pathMatchesSubscription(path, configured.replace(/^change./, \"\"));\n      }\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    let runtimeEventQueue = [];\n    let runtimeEventProcessing = false;\n    let runtimeRenderRequested = false;\n\n    function requestRuntimeRender() {\n      runtimeRenderRequested = true;\n    }\n\n    function routeRuntimeEvent(name, detail = {}) {\n      if (!name || !findState(current)) return false;\n      if (detail?.source === \"fetch\" && detail?.type === \"change\" && detail.value && (detail.value.loading === true || detail.value.status === \"loading\" || detail.value.done === false)) {\n        updateActionButtons();\n        refreshBlockedValidation();\n        notifyRuntime();\n        return false;\n      }\n      const candidates = [];\n      const invalidContracts = [];\n      candidateStatesForRuntimeEvent(name, detail).forEach(state => {\n        const stateOutgoing = outgoing(state.id);\n        const contract = runtimeTransitionTriggerContract(stateOutgoing);\n        if (!contract.ok) {\n          invalidContracts.push({ state, contract });\n          return;\n        }\n        stateOutgoing.forEach(transition => {\n          if (transitionMatchesRuntimeEvent(transition, name, detail)) candidates.push({ state, transition });\n        });\n      });\n      if (invalidContracts.length) {\n        const invalid = invalidContracts[0];\n        runtimeValidation = {\n          type: \"invalid-trigger-contract\",\n          stateId: invalid.state.id,\n          eventName: name,\n          reason: invalid.contract.reason,\n          transitionIds: invalid.contract.transitionIds,\n          message: \"Die Trigger dieses Zustands sind nicht eindeutig.\"\n        };\n        blockedTransitionId = null;\n        requestRuntimeRender();\n        return false;\n      }\n      const eligible = candidates.filter(item => conditionOk(item.transition.condition));\n      if (eligible.length === 1) {\n        followTransition(eligible[0].transition, eligible[0].state.id, name);\n        requestRuntimeRender();\n        return true;\n      }\n      if (eligible.length > 1) {\n        runtimeValidation = {\n          type: \"ambiguous-transition\",\n          stateId: current,\n          eventName: name,\n          transitionIds: eligible.map(item => item.transition.id),\n          message: \"Mehrere Übergänge passen auf dasselbe Ereignis.\"\n        };\n        blockedTransitionId = null;\n        requestRuntimeRender();\n        return false;\n      }\n      const shouldRenderBlock = candidates.some(item => [\"button\", \"event\"].includes(transitionTriggerType(item.transition)));\n      if (shouldRenderBlock && candidates[0]) {\n        const transition = candidates[0].transition;\n        runtimeValidation = {\n          type: \"blocked-transition\",\n          transitionId: transition.id,\n          message: transition.condition\n            ? \"Bedingung nicht erfüllt: \" + transition.condition\n            : \"Kein passender Übergang ist verfügbar.\"\n        };\n        blockedTransitionId = transition.id;\n        requestRuntimeRender();\n      } else {\n        updateActionButtons();\n        refreshBlockedValidation();\n        notifyRuntime();\n      }\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function flushRuntimeEvents() {\n      let handled = false;\n      while (runtimeEventQueue.length) {\n        const event = runtimeEventQueue.shift();\n        handled = routeRuntimeEvent(event.name, event.detail) || handled;\n      }\n      return handled;\n    }\n\n    function emitRuntimeEvent(eventName, detail = {}, realtimeEventConfig = null) {\n      if (runtimeEventDetailRequiresTrustedUiEvent(detail) && !runtimeUiCommitIsTrusted()) return false;\n      if (runtimeEventDetailRequiresTrustedUiEvent(detail)) runtimeRecorderLeaveReplayMode();\n      if (runtimeIsPaused()) {\n        notifyRuntime();\n        return false;\n      }\n      const name = recordRuntimeEvent(eventName, detail, realtimeEventConfig);\n      if (!name) return false;\n      if (runtimeRecording.active && !runtimeRecording.playing && runtimeRecorderShouldCaptureEvent(name, detail)) {\n        runtimeRecorderCaptureFrame(\"event\", { eventName: name, detail });\n      }\n      if (name.startsWith(\"realtime.\")) applyRealtimeEventBindings(name, detail, realtimeEventConfig);\n      runtimeEventQueue.push({ name, detail: { ...detail, eventName: name } });\n      if (runtimeEventProcessing) return true;\n      runtimeEventProcessing = true;\n      let handled = false;\n      try {\n        handled = flushRuntimeEvents();\n      } finally {\n        runtimeEventProcessing = false;\n        if (runtimeRenderRequested) {\n          runtimeRenderRequested = false;\n          render();\n        }\n      }\n      return handled;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeSet(path, value, opts = {}) {\n      const source = runtimeWriteSource(opts);\n      if (!runtimeWriteSourceIsAuthorized(opts)) {\n        throw new Error(\"Unauthorized runtime bus write source: \" + source);\n      }\n      if (runtimeWriteSourceRequiresTrustedUiEvent(source) && !runtimeUiCommitIsTrusted()) {\n        if (typeof render === \"function\") render();\n        return false;\n      }\n      const write = writeRuntimeState(path, value, { ...opts, token: RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN });\n      const targetPath = write.path;\n      if (!targetPath) return false;\n      if (!write.changed || opts.silent) {\n        if (opts.notify !== false) notifyRuntime();\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (runtimeWriteSourceRequiresTrustedUiEvent(source)) {\n        runtimeRecorderLeaveReplayMode({ renderLive: true });\n        runtimeRecorderCaptureFrame(\"input\", { path: targetPath, value: write.value });\n      }\n      const eventName = normalizeTransitionEvent(opts.eventName || \"change.\" + targetPath);\n      const detail = { type: \"change\", path: targetPath, value: write.value, source: opts.source || \"runtime\" };\n      if (write.previous !== undefined) detail.previous = write.previous;\n      return emitRuntimeEvent(eventName, detail);\n    }\n\n    function setRuntimeCurrent(nextId, source = \"runtime\", silent = true) {\n      const next = String(nextId || \"\");\n      const previous = current || \"\";\n      const busPrevious = readValueAtPath(context, \"state.current\") || \"\";\n      current = next;\n      if (previous === next && busPrevious === next) return false;\n      return runtimeSet(\"state.current\", next, { source, silent, notify: false, eventName: \"change.state.current\" });\n    }\n\n    function syncRuntimeCurrent(source = \"runtime\", silent = true) {\n      return setRuntimeCurrent(current || \"\", source, silent);\n    }\n\n    function readContextPathRaw(path) {\n      const targetPath = runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(path, \"\");\n      for (let i = renderContextScopes.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {\n        const scopedValue = readValueAtPath(renderContextScopes[i], targetPath);\n        if (scopedValue !== undefined) return scopedValue;\n      }\n      return readValueAtPath(context, targetPath);\n    }\n\n    function renderLiteralText(text) {\n      return String(text || \"\");\n    }\n\n\n    function childStates(parentId) {\n      return model.states\n        .filter(state => (state.parentId || null) === parentId)\n        .sort((a, b) => (a.y - b.y) || (a.x - b.x) || String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id)));\n    }\n\n    function flowChildStates(parentId) {\n      return childStates(parentId);\n    }\n\n    function boundaryConfigFor(state) {\n      const boundary = state && typeof state.boundary === \"object\" ? state.boundary : {};\n      return {\n        entryId: String(boundary.entryId || \"\"),\n        exitId: String(boundary.exitId || \"\"),\n        entryDisabled: Boolean(boundary.entryDisabled),\n        exitDisabled: Boolean(boundary.exitDisabled)\n      };\n    }\n\n    function groupEntryState(parentId, transition = null) {\n      const children = flowChildStates(parentId);\n      if (!children.length) return null;\n      if (transition?.groupEntryId) {\n        return children.find(state => state.id === transition.groupEntryId) || null;\n      }\n      const boundary = boundaryConfigFor(findState(parentId));\n      if (boundary.entryDisabled) return null;\n      if (boundary.entryId) return children.find(state => state.id === boundary.entryId) || null;\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    function resolveRuntimeTarget(targetId, transition = null) {\n      const target = findState(targetId);\n      if (!target) return targetId;\n      let currentTarget = target;\n      let currentTransition = transition;\n      const seen = new Set();\n      while (currentTarget && !seen.has(currentTarget.id)) {\n        seen.add(currentTarget.id);\n        const entry = groupEntryState(currentTarget.id, currentTransition);\n        if (!entry) return currentTarget.id;\n        currentTarget = entry;\n        currentTransition = null;\n      }\n      return currentTarget?.id || target.id;\n    }\n\n    function cloneRuntimeDefaultValue(value) {\n      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));\n    }\n\n    function mergeStateDefaultValue(existing, nextValue) {\n      if (existing === undefined) return { changed: true, value: cloneRuntimeDefaultValue(nextValue) };\n      if (Array.isArray(existing) && Array.isArray(nextValue)) {\n        let changed = false;\n        const next = existing.slice();\n        nextValue.forEach((child, index) => {\n          const merged = mergeStateDefaultValue(next[index], child);\n          if (merged.changed) {\n            next[index] = merged.value;\n            changed = true;\n          }\n        });\n        return { changed, value: next };\n      }\n      if (!isPlainDataObject(existing) || !isPlainDataObject(nextValue)) return { changed: false, value: existing };\n      let changed = false;\n      const next = { ...existing };\n      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(nextValue)) {\n        const merged = mergeStateDefaultValue(next[key], child);\n        if (merged.changed) {\n          next[key] = merged.value;\n          changed = true;\n        }\n      }\n      return { changed, value: next };\n    }\n\n    function applyMissingStateDefault(path, value) {\n      const merged = mergeStateDefaultValue(readValueAtPath(context, path), value);\n      if (!merged.changed) return;\n      runtimeSet(path, merged.value, { source: \"state-default\", notify: false, eventName: \"change.\" + path });\n    }\n\n    function runtimeValuesEqual(a, b) {\n      if (a === b) return true;\n      if (Array.isArray(a) || Array.isArray(b)) {\n        if (!Array.isArray(a) || !Array.isArray(b) || a.length !== b.length) return false;\n        return a.every((value, index) => runtimeValuesEqual(value, b[index]));\n      }\n      if (isPlainDataObject(a) || isPlainDataObject(b)) {\n        if (!isPlainDataObject(a) || !isPlainDataObject(b)) return false;\n        const aKeys = Object.keys(a).sort();\n        const bKeys = Object.keys(b).sort();\n        if (aKeys.length !== bKeys.length) return false;\n        return aKeys.every((key, index) => key === bKeys[index] && runtimeValuesEqual(a[key], b[key]));\n      }\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function mergeDesignerDefaultOverride(currentValue, previousDefault, nextDefault) {\n      if (currentValue === undefined || runtimeValuesEqual(currentValue, previousDefault)) {\n        return { changed: !runtimeValuesEqual(currentValue, nextDefault), value: cloneRuntimeDefaultValue(nextDefault) };\n      }\n      if (Array.isArray(currentValue) && Array.isArray(previousDefault) && Array.isArray(nextDefault)) {\n        const next = [];\n        let changed = currentValue.length !== nextDefault.length;\n        for (let index = 0; index < nextDefault.length; index += 1) {\n          const merged = mergeDesignerDefaultOverride(currentValue[index], previousDefault[index], nextDefault[index]);\n          next[index] = merged.value;\n          if (merged.changed || !runtimeValuesEqual(currentValue[index], next[index])) changed = true;\n        }\n        return { changed, value: next };\n      }\n      if (isPlainDataObject(currentValue) && isPlainDataObject(previousDefault) && isPlainDataObject(nextDefault)) {\n        const next = {};\n        const currentKeys = Object.keys(currentValue);\n        const nextKeys = Object.keys(nextDefault);\n        let changed = currentKeys.length !== nextKeys.length || currentKeys.some(key => !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(nextDefault, key));\n        for (const key of nextKeys) {\n          const merged = mergeDesignerDefaultOverride(currentValue[key], previousDefault[key], nextDefault[key]);\n          next[key] = merged.value;\n          if (merged.changed || !runtimeValuesEqual(currentValue[key], next[key])) changed = true;\n        }\n        return { changed, value: next };\n      }\n      return { changed: false, value: currentValue };\n    }\n\n    function applyDesignerStateDataDefaultOverrides(previousModel, nextModel, stateIds) {\n      const ids = [...new Set((Array.isArray(stateIds) ? stateIds : []).map(id => String(id || \"\").trim()).filter(Boolean))];\n      if (!ids.length) return;\n      const previousStates = new Map((previousModel?.states || []).map(state => [String(state.id || \"\"), state]));\n      const nextStates = new Map((nextModel?.states || []).map(state => [String(state.id || \"\"), state]));\n      for (const stateId of ids) {\n        const state = nextStates.get(stateId);\n        if (!state) continue;\n        const previousData = normalizeStateDataObject(previousStates.get(stateId)?.data);\n        const nextData = normalizeStateDataObject(state.data);\n        const scope = \"states.\" + state.id;\n        for (const [key, nextValue] of Object.entries(nextData)) {\n          const path = scope + \".\" + key;\n          const merged = mergeDesignerDefaultOverride(readValueAtPath(context, path), previousData[key], nextValue);\n          if (merged.changed) runtimeSet(path, merged.value, { source: \"state-default\", notify: false, eventName: \"change.\" + path });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function applyStateDataDefaults(state) {\n      const scope = \"states.\" + state.id;\n      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(normalizeStateDataObject(state.data))) {\n        applyMissingStateDefault(scope + \".\" + key, value);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function applyActiveStateDataDefaults(state) {\n      for (const activeState of activeStateChain(state)) applyStateDataDefaults(activeState);\n    }\n\n    function applyTransitionSet(transition) {\n      for (const [path, value] of Object.entries(normalizeDataObject(transition.set))) {\n        runtimeSet(path, value, { source: \"transition\", eventName: \"change.\" + normalizeContextPath(path, \"\") });\n      }\n    }\n\n    function enterState(nextId, transition = null) {\n      const runtimeTarget = resolveRuntimeTarget(nextId, transition);\n      if (!findState(runtimeTarget)) return false;\n      setRuntimeCurrent(runtimeTarget, \"flow\", true);\n      stateActivationId += 1;\n      suppressNextFocus = true;\n      suppressActionUntil = 0;\n      blockedTransitionId = null;\n      runtimeValidation = null;\n      if (transition) transitionToNotify = { ...transition, runtimeTo: runtimeTarget };\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function findState(id) { return model.__stateById?.get?.(id) || model.states.find(s => s.id === id); }\n    function stateById(id) { return findState(id) || null; }\n    function isBoundaryProxyReference(id) { return typeof id === \"string\" && id.startsWith(\"proxy:\"); }\n    function isOutputBoundaryFlow(transition) {\n      return transition?.boundaryFlow?.side === \"output\" || isBoundaryProxyReference(transition?.to);\n    }\n    function outputBoundaryFlowForState(parent, child) {\n      if (!parent || !child) return null;\n      return model.transitions.find(t =>\n        isOutputBoundaryFlow(t) &&\n        (t.boundaryFlow?.parentId ? t.boundaryFlow.parentId === parent.id : true) &&\n        (t.boundaryFlow?.stateId ? t.boundaryFlow.stateId === child.id : true) &&\n        t.from === child.id\n      ) || {\n        ...transition,\n        from: activeState.id,\n        runtimeParentExit: true,\n        runtimeParentFrom: parent.id\n      };\n    }\n\n    function childCanExitParent(child, parent) {\n      if (!child || !parent) return false;\n      if (outputBoundaryFlowForState(parent, child)) return true;\n      const boundary = boundaryConfigFor(parent);\n      if (boundary.exitDisabled && function isBoundaryExitState(state) {\n      if (!state?.parentId) return false;\n      const parent = findState(state.parentId);\n      if (!parent) return false;\n      if \"\"].join(\":\");\n          if (seen.has(key)) continue;\n          seen.add(key);\n          out.push(transition);\n        }\n      }\n      return out;\n    }\n\n    function outgoing(id) {\n function runtimeIsPaused() {\n      return readValueAtPath(context, \"runtime.paused\") === true;\n    }\n\n    function declaredBusVariables() {\n      const variables = [];\n      const visit =    ? \"number\"\n          : \"text\";\n        variables.push({\n          name: \"states.\" + state.id + \".\" + localPath,\n          type,\n          kind: declaredType === \"float\" ? \"float\" :        const raw = String(value);\n        if (v.kind === \"int\") return /^\\d+$/.test(raw);\n        return /^\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?$/.test(raw);\n      }\n      if (v.type === \"text\") return value !== null && value !== undefined;\n      return typeof value === \"boolean\";\n    }\n\n    function strictNumber(value) {\n      if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;\n      if (typeof value === \"number\") return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;\n      const raw = String(value).trim();\n      if (!/^-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?$/.test(raw)) return null;\n      const n = Number(raw);\n      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;\n    }\n\n    function coerceValue(text) {\n      const raw = String(text).trim().replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, \"\");\n      if (raw === \"true\") return true;\n      if (raw === \"false\") return false;\n      if (raw === \"null\" || raw === \"undefined\") return Symbol.for(\"invalid\");\n      if (/^-?\\d+(\\.\\d+)?$/.test(raw)) return Number(raw);\n      return raw;\n    }\n\n    function testAtom(atom) {\n      atom = atom.trim();\n      if (!atom || atom === \"true\") return true;\n      if (atom === \"false\") return false;\n\n      if (atom.startsWith(\"!\")) {\n        const name = atom.slice(1).trim();\n        const value = readContextPathRaw(name);\n        if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;\n        return !Boolean(value);\n      }\n\n      const m = atom.match(/^([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\\.(?:[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*|\\d+))*)\\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\\s*(.+)$/);\n      if (!m) {\n        const value = readContextPathRaw(atom);\n        const v = variableMap().get(atom);\n        if (v && !isValidValue(value, v)) return false;\n        return Boolean(value);\n      }\n\n      const left = readContextPathRaw(m[1]);\n      const op = m[2];\n      const right = coerceValue(m[3]);\n      const vars = variableMap();\n      const v = vars.get(m[1]);\n      if (left === null || left === undefined || right === Symbol.for(\"invalid\")) return false;\n      if (v && !isValidValue(left, v)) return false;\n\n      if ([\">=\", \"<=\", \">\", \"<\"].includes(op)) {\n        const l = strictNumber(left);\n        const r = typeof right === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(right) ? right : null;\n        if (l === null || r === null) return false;\n        if (op === \">=\") return l >= r;\n        if (op === \"<=\") return l <= r;\n        if (op === \">\") return l > r;\n        if (op === \"<\") return l < r;\n      }\n      if (typeof right === \"number\") {\n        const l = strictNumber(left);\n        if (l === null) return false;\n        return op === \"==\" ? l === right : l !== right;\n      }\n      if (typeof right === \"boolean\") {\n        if (typeof left !== \"boolean\") return false;\n        return op === \"==\" ? left === right : left !== right;\n      }\n      return op === \"==\" ? String(left) === String(right) : String(left) !== String(right);\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function conditionOk(condition) {\n      const c = String(condition || \"\").trim();\n      if (!c) return true;\n      return c.split(\"||\").some(orPart => orPart.split(\"&&\").every(testAtom));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeStateTitle(id) {\n      const state = findState(id);\n      return String(state?.title || id || \"\");\n    }\n\n\n    const RUNTIME_RECORDER_MAX_FRAMES = 240;\n    const RUNTIME_RECORDER_STEP_MS = 650;\n    const RUNTIME_RECORDER_MIN_STEP_MS = 120;\n    const RUNTIME_RECORDER_MAX_STEP_MS = 3500;\n    const runtimeRecording = {\n      active: false,\n      mode: \"live\",\n      playing: false,\n      timer: 0,\n      scrollTimer: 0,\n      index: -1,\n      frames: [],\n      pendingFrame: null,\n      startedAt: 0,\n      lastFrameAt: 0,\n      createdAt: \"\",\n      model: null,\n      loop: false\n    };\n\n    // Isolated UI replay artifact; never part of the persisted model or transition contract.\n    function runtimeRecorderClone(value) {\n      if (value === undefined) return undefined;\n      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderNowMs() {\n      return typeof performance !== \"undefined\" && typeof performance.now === \"function\" ? function runtimeRecorderTruncateCanvasText(ctx, text, maxWidth) {\n      let value = String(text || \"\").replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n      if (!value || ctx.measureText(value).width <= maxWidth) runtimeRecorderDrawRoundedRect(ctx, x, y, width, height, radius, fill, stroke = \"\", lineWidth = 1) {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      if (typeof ctx.roundRect === \"function\") ctx.roundRect(x, y, width, height, radius);\n      else {\n        ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(x + width - radius, y);\n        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + width, y, x + width, y + radius);\n        ctx.lineTo(x + width, y + height - radius);\n        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + width, y + height, x + width - radius, y + height);\n        ctx.lineTo(x + radius, y + height);\n        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + height, x, y + height - radius);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y + radius);\n        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + radius, y);\n      }\n      if (fill) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = fill;\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n      if (stroke) {\n        ctx.strokeStyle = stroke;\n        ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifFrameDelayMs(frame) {\n      const value = Number(frame?.delayMs);\n      if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) return Math.max(80, Math.min(2200, Math.round(value)));\n      const kind = String(frame?.kind || \"\");\n      if (kind === \"start\") return 650;\n      if (kind === \"transition\" || kind === \"event\") return 900;\n      if (kind === \"input\") return 180;\n      return RUNTIME_RECORDER_STEP_MS;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifFrames(payload = {}) {\n      const frames = Array.isArray(payload.frames) ? payload.frames.filter(Boolean) : [];\n      const sampled = frames.length <= 80\n        ? frames\n        : Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, index) => frames[Math.round(index * (frames.length - 1) / 79)]);\n      return sampled.map((frame, index) => ({\n        ...runtimeRecorderClone(frame),\n        index: index + 1,\n        delayMs: runtimeRecorderGifFrameDelayMs(frame)\n      }));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifSafeScreenFragment(frame) {\n      const template = document.createElement(\"template\");\n      template.innerHTML = String(frame?.screen?.html || \"\");\n      template.content.querySelectorAll(\"script, style, iframe, object, embed\").forEach(el => el.remove());\n      template.content.querySelectorAll(\".runtime-record-marker.event, .runtime-record-marker.transition\").forEach(el => el.remove());\n      template.content.querySelectorAll(\"img\").forEach(img => {\n        const src = String(img.getAttribute(\"src\") || \"-1\");\n      });\n      return template.content.cloneNode(true);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderBuildGifFrameElement(payload, frame, width, height) {\n      const screenSnapshot = const currentTitle = runtimeRecorderPayloadStateTitle(payload, frame?.current) || String(frame?.current || \"\");\n      const maxScreenHeight = Math.max(220, height - 70);\n      const root = 34%),radial-gradient(circle at 90% 8%, rgba(245,158,11,.14), transparent 30%),linear-gradient(180deg,#07111f 0%,#020617 58%,#01040b 100%);font-family:'Atkinson Hyperlegible','Aptos','Segoe UI Variable','Trebuchet MS',sans-serif;color:#e5f0ff;pointer-events:none;\";\n      const top = document.createElement(\"div\");\n      top.className = \"top\";\n      top.style.cssText = \"height:34px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin:0 0 10px;\";\n      const title = document.createElement(\"div\");\n      title.className = \"title\";\n top.appendChild(pill);\n      root.appendChild(top);\n      const screen = document.createElement(\"main\");\n      screen.id = \"screen\";\n      screen.className = className;\n      screen.dataset.replayFrame = \"true\";\n      screen.style.width = \"100%\";\n      screen.style.maxHeight = maxScreenHeight + \"px\";\n      screen.style.overflow = \"hidden\";\n      screen.style.margin = \"0\";\n      screen.style.borderRadius = \"18px\";\n      screen.style.padding = className.includes(\"widget-screen\") ? \"0\" : \"24px\";\n      const scroll =
