  return root;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderCssIsVisibleColor(value) {\n      const color = String(value || \"\").trim();\n      return Boolean(color && color !== \"transparent\" && !/^rgba?\\(\\s*0\\s*,\\s*0\\s*,\\s*0\\s*,\\s*0\\s*\\)$/i.test(color));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderCssNumber(value, fallback = 0) {\n      const parsed = Number.parseFloat(String(value || \"\"));\n      return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : fallback;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifRelativeRect(rect, rootRect, width, height) {\n      const x = Math.max(-width, Math.min(width * 2, rect.left - rootRect.left));\n      const y = Math.max(-height, Math.min(height * 2, rect.top - rootRect.top));\n      return { x, y, width: rect.width, height: rect.height };\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderDrawDomBox(ctx, el, rootRect, width, height) {\n      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;\n      const box = runtimeRecorderGifRelativeRect(rect, rootRect, width, height);\n      if (box.x > width || box.y > height || box.x + box.width < 0 || box.y + box.height < 0) return;\n      const style = getComputedStyle(el);\n      const radius = Math.min(Math.min(box.width, box.height) / 2, runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.borderTopLeftRadius, 0));\n      if (runtimeRecorderCssIsVisibleColor(style.backgroundColor)) {\n        runtimeRecorderDrawRoundedRect(ctx, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height, radius, style.backgroundColor, \"\", 0);\n      }\n      const borderWidth = Math.max(runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.borderTopWidth, 0), runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.borderRightWidth, 0), runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.borderBottomWidth, 0), runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.borderLeftWidth, 0));\n      if (borderWidth > 0 && runtimeRecorderCssIsVisibleColor(style.borderTopColor)) {\n        runtimeRecorderDrawRoundedRect(ctx, box.x + borderWidth / 2, box.y + borderWidth / 2, Math.max(0, box.width - borderWidth), Math.max(0, box.height - borderWidth), radius, \"\", style.borderTopColor, borderWidth);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderDrawDomText(ctx, node, rootRect, width, height) {\n      const text = String(node.nodeValue || \"\").replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n      if (!text.trim()) return;\n      const parent = node.parentElement;\n      if (!parent) return;\n      const style = getComputedStyle(parent);\n      if (style.visibility === \"hidden\" || style.display === \"none\" || !runtimeRecorderCssIsVisibleColor(style.color)) return;\n      const range = document.createRange();\n      range.selectNodeContents(node);\n      const rects = Array.from(range.getClientRects());\n      range.detach();\n      if (!rects.length) return;\n      const fontSize = runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.fontSize, 14);\n      ctx.fillStyle = style.color;\n      ctx.font = [style.fontStyle, style.fontVariant, style.fontWeight, style.fontSize, style.fontFamily].filter(Boolean).join(\" \");\n      ctx.textBaseline = \"alphabetic\";\n      rects.slice(0, 3).forEach((rect, index) => {\n        const box = runtimeRecorderGifRelativeRect(rect, rootRect, width, height);\n        if (box.x > width || box.y > height || box.x + box.width < 0 || box.y + box.height < 0) return;\n        const line = index === 0 ? text.trim() : \"\";\n        if (line) ctx.fillText(runtimeRecorderTruncateCanvasText(ctx, line, Math.max(24, width - box.x - 16)), box.x, box.y + fontSize * .86);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderDrawDomControlValue(ctx, el, rootRect, width, height) {\n      const tag = String(el.tagName || \"\").toLowerCase();\n      if (tag !== \"input\" && tag !== \"textarea\" && tag !== \"select\") return;\n      const value = tag === \"select\"\n        ? String(el.options?.[el.selectedIndex]?.text || el.value || \"\")\n        : String(el.value || el.getAttribute(\"value\") || \"\");\n      if (!value) return;\n      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;\n      const box = runtimeRecorderGifRelativeRect(rect, rootRect, width, height);\n      const style = getComputedStyle(el);\n      const fontSize = runtimeRecorderCssNumber(style.fontSize, 14);\n      ctx.fillStyle = runtimeRecorderCssIsVisibleColor(style.color) ? style.color : \"#e5f0ff\";\n      ctx.font = [style.fontStyle, style.fontVariant, style.fontWeight, style.fontSize, style.fontFamily].filter(Boolean).join(\" \");\n      ctx.textBaseline = \"alphabetic\";\n      ctx.fillText(runtimeRecorderTruncateCanvasText(ctx, value, Math.max(24, box.width - 18)), box.x + 10, box.y + Math.max(fontSize + 8, box.height / 2 + fontSize * .35));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderDrawDomFrame(ctx, root, width, height) {\n      const rootRect = root.getBoundingClientRect();\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#020617\";\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n      const elements = [root, ...root.querySelectorAll(\"*\")].filter(el => {\n        const style = getComputedStyle(el);\n        return style.display !== \"none\" && style.visibility !== \"hidden\";\n      });\n      elements.forEach(el => runtimeRecorderDrawDomBox(ctx, el, rootRect, width, height));\n      const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);\n      while (walker.nextNode()) runtimeRecorderDrawDomText(ctx, walker.currentNode, rootRect, width, height);\n      root.querySelectorAll(\"input, textarea, select\").forEach(el => runtimeRecorderDrawDomControlValue(ctx, el, rootRect, width, height));\n    }\n\n    async function runtimeRecorderDrawGifFrame(ctx, payload, frame) {\n      const width = ctx.canvas.width;\n      const height = ctx.canvas.height;\n      const root = runtimeRecorderBuildGifFrameElement(payload, frame, width, height);\n      document.body.appendChild(root);\n      await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;\n      runtimeRecorderDrawDomFrame(ctx, root, width, height);\n      ctx.restore();\n      root.remove();\n    }\n\n    const RUNTIME_RECORDER_GIF_BASE_COLORS = [\n      [2, 6, 23], [6, 17, 31], [8, 24, 39], [15, 23, 42], [29, 57, 86], [49, 95, 140],\n      [56, 189, 248], [14, 165, 233], [125, 211, 252], [224, 242, 254], [248, 250, 252], [226, 232, 240],\n      [203, 213, 225], [148, 163, 184], [100, 116, 139], [52, 211, 153], [34, 197, 94], [6, 78, 59],\n      [5, 46, 33], [187, 247, 208], [245, 158, 11], [253, 230, 138], [251, 113, 133], [255, 228, 230],\n      [3, 16, 31], [8, 47, 73], [12, 74, 110], [30, 41, 59], [51, 65, 85], [0, 0, 0], [255, 255, 255]\n    ];\n    let runtimeRecorderGifCachedPaletteColors = null;\n    const runtimeRecorderGifColorCache = new Map();\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifPaletteColors() {\n      if (runtimeRecorderGifCachedPaletteColors) return runtimeRecorderGifCachedPaletteColors;\n      const colors = [];\n      const seen = new Set();\n      const add = (r, g, b) => {\n        const color = [Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(r))), Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(g))), Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(b)))];\n        const key = color.join(\",\");\n        if (seen.has(key) || colors.length >= 256) return;\n        seen.add(key);\n        colors.push(color);\n      };\n      RUNTIME_RECORDER_GIF_BASE_COLORS.forEach(color => add(color[0], color[1], color[2]));\n      const levels = [0, 51, 102, 153, 204, 255];\n      for (const r of levels) for (const g of levels) for (const b of levels) add(r, g, b);\n      while (colors.length < 256) colors.push([0, 0, 0]);\n      runtimeRecorderGifCachedPaletteColors = colors.slice(0, 256);\n      return runtimeRecorderGifCachedPaletteColors;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifPalette() {\n      return runtimeRecorderGifPaletteColors().flat();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifColorIndex(r, g, b, a) {\n      if (a < 128) return 0;\n      const rr = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(r)));\n      const gg = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(g)));\n      const bb = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(b)));\n      const cacheKey = ((rr >> 3) << 10) | ((gg >> 3) << 5) | (bb >> 3);\n      const cached = runtimeRecorderGifColorCache.get(cacheKey);\n      if (cached !== undefined) return cached;\n      const colors = runtimeRecorderGifPaletteColors();\n      let bestIndex = 0;\n      let bestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\n      for (let index = 0; index < colors.length; index += 1) {\n        const color = colors[index];\n        const dr = rr - color[0];\n        const dg = gg - color[1];\n        const db = bb - color[2];\n        const distance = dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db;\n        if (distance < bestDistance) {\n          bestDistance = distance;\n          bestIndex = index;\n          if (distance === 0) break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (runtimeRecorderGifColorCache.size > 32768) runtimeRecorderGifColorCache.clear();\n      runtimeRecorderGifColorCache.set(cacheKey, bestIndex);\n      return bestIndex;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifIndices(ctx) {\n      const image = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, ctx.canvas.width, ctx.canvas.height).data;\n      const indices = new Uint8Array(ctx.canvas.width * ctx.canvas.height);\n      for (let i = 0, p = 0; i < image.length; i += 4, p += 1) {\n        indices[p] = runtimeRecorderGifColorIndex(image[i], image[i + 1], image[i + 2], image[i + 3]);\n      }\n      return indices;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifLzw(indices, minCodeSize = 8) {\n      const clearCode = 1 << minCodeSize;\n      const endCode = clearCode + 1;\n      let codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;\n      let nextCode = endCode + 1;\n      let bitBuffer = 0;\n      let bitCount = 0;\n      const bytes = [];\n      const dict = new Map();\n      const resetDict = () => {\n        dict.clear();\n        for (let i = 0; i < clearCode; i += 1) dict.set(String(i), i);\n        codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;\n        nextCode = endCode + 1;\n      };\n      const writeCode = code => {\n        bitBuffer |= code << bitCount;\n        bitCount += codeSize;\n        while (bitCount >= 8) {\n          bytes.push(bitBuffer & 255);\n          bitBuffer >>= 8;\n          bitCount -= 8;\n        }\n      };\n      resetDict();\n      writeCode(clearCode);\n      let prefix = String(indices[0] || 0);\n      for (let i = 1; i < indices.length; i += 1) {\n        const value = indices[i];\n        const key = prefix + \",\" + value;\n        if (dict.has(key)) {\n          prefix = key;\n          continue;\n        }\n        writeCode(dict.get(prefix));\n        if (nextCode < 4096) {\n          dict.set(key, nextCode++);\n          if (nextCode > (1 << codeSize) && codeSize < 12) codeSize += 1;\n        } else {\n          writeCode(clearCode);\n          resetDict();\n        }\n        prefix = String(value);\n      }\n      writeCode(dict.get(prefix));\n      writeCode(endCode);\n      if (bitCount > 0) bytes.push(bitBuffer & 255);\n      return bytes;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderGifSubBlocks(bytes, out) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 255) {\n        const chunk = bytes.slice(i, i + 255);\n        out.push(chunk.length, ...chunk);\n      }\n      out.push(0);\n    }\n\n    async function runtimeRecorderGifBytes(payload) {\n      const width = 640;\n      const height = 360;\n      const gifFrames = runtimeRecorderGifFrames(payload);\n      const gifPayload = { ...payload, frames: gifFrames, frameCount: gifFrames.length };\n      const canvas = document.createElement(\"canvas\");\n      canvas.width = width;\n      canvas.height = height;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext(\"2d\", { willReadFrequently: true });\n      if (!ctx) return null;\n      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;\n      const out = [];\n      const writeText = text => { for (const ch of text) out.push(ch.charCodeAt(0)); };\n      const writeU16 = value => { out.push(value & 255, (value >> 8) & 255); };\n      writeText(\"GIF89a\");\n      writeU16(width);\n      writeU16(height);\n      out.push(0xF7, 0, 0);\n      out.push(...runtimeRecorderGifPalette());\n      out.push(0x21, 0xFF, 11);\n      writeText(\"NETSCAPE2.0\");\n      out.push(3, 1, 0, 0, 0);\n      for (const frame of gifFrames) {\n        await runtimeRecorderDrawGifFrame(ctx, gifPayload, frame);\n        const delay = Math.max(8, Math.min(350, Math.round((Number(frame.delayMs) || RUNTIME_RECORDER_STEP_MS) / 10)));\n        out.push(0x21, 0xF9, 4, 0);\n        writeU16(delay);\n        out.push(0, 0);\n        out.push(0x2C);\n        writeU16(0);\n        writeU16(0);\n        writeU16(width);\n        writeU16(height);\n        out.push(0);\n        out.push(8);\n        runtimeRecorderGifSubBlocks(runtimeRecorderGifLzw(runtimeRecorderGifIndices(ctx), 8), out);\n      }\n      out.push(0x3B);\n      return new Uint8Array(out);\n    }\n\n\n    async function runtimeRecorderDownloadGif(payload, safeName) {\n      try {\n        const bytes = await runtimeRecorderGifBytes(payload);\n        if (!bytes || !bytes.length) return false;\n        return runtimeRecorderDownloadBlob(new Blob([bytes], { type: \"image/gif\" }), safeName + \"-aufnahme.gif\");\n      } catch (_) {\n        return false;\n      }\n    }\n\n\n    async function runtimeRecorderDownload() {\n      if (!runtimeRecording.frames.length) return false;\n      const payload = runtimeRecorderExportPayload();\n      const safeName = runtimeRecorderSafeFileName(payload.appName);\n      runtimeRecorderDownloadBlob(new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: \"application/json\" }), safeName + \"-aufnahme.json\");\n      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 0));\n      await runtimeRecorderDownloadGif(payload, safeName);\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderIsReplaying() {\n      return runtimeRecording.mode === \"replay\" || runtimeRecording.playing === true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderFrameLabel(kind, detail = {}) {\n      const transition = detail.transition || null;\n      if (transition) {\n        const label = runtimeTransitionLabel(transition);\n        const target = runtimeStateTitle(detail.to || transition.to || \"\");\n        return target ? label + \" -> \" + target : label;\n      }\n      if (kind === \"click\") return runtimeRecorderMarkerLabel(\"click\", runtimeRecorderFrameDetail(kind, detail));\n      if (kind === \"scroll\") return \"Scroll\";\n      if (kind === \"input\") return String(detail.path || \"Eingabe\");\n      if (kind === \"event\") return \"Ereignis: \" + String(detail.eventName || detail.detail?.eventName || \"\");\n      if (kind === \"start\") return \"Start\";\n      return runtimeStateTitle(current) || String(current || \"\");\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderFrameSignature(frame) {\n      return String(frame?.kind || \"\") + \"\\u0000\" + String(frame?.current || \"\") + \"\\u0000\" + JSON.stringify(frame?.context || {}) + \"\\u0000\" + JSON.stringify(frame?.detail || {}) + \"\\u0000\" + String(frame?.screen?.html || \"\");\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderCaptureFrame(kind = \"snapshot\", detail = {}) {\n      if (!runtimeRecording.active || runtimeRecording.playing) return null;\n      const nowMs = runtimeRecorderNowMs();\n      if (!runtimeRecording.startedAt) runtimeRecording.startedAt = nowMs;\n      if (!runtimeRecording.createdAt) runtimeRecording.createdAt = new Date().toISOString();\n      if (!runtimeRecording.model) runtimeRecording.model = runtimeRecorderClone(model);\n      const previous = runtimeRecording.frames[runtimeRecording.frames.length - 1];\n      const frameDetail = runtimeRecorderFrameDetail(kind, detail);\n      frameDetail.kind = String(kind || \"snapshot\");\n      const frame = {\n        index: runtimeRecording.frames.length + 1,\n        at: new Date().toISOString(),\n        atMs: Math.max(0, Math.round(nowMs - runtimeRecording.startedAt)),\n        delayMs: previous ? Math.max(0, Math.round(nowMs - (runtimeRecording.lastFrameAt || nowMs))) : 0,\n        kind: String(kind || \"snapshot\"),\n        current: String(current || readValueAtPath(context, \"state.current\") || \"\"),\n        label: runtimeRecorderFrameLabel(kind, detail),\n        transitionId: String(detail.transition?.id || detail.transitionId || frameDetail.transitionId || \"\"),\n        triggerEvent: normalizeTransitionEvent(detail.triggerEvent || frameDetail.eventName || \"\"),\n        eventName: normalizeTransitionEvent(detail.eventName || frameDetail.eventName || \"\"),\n        detail: frameDetail,\n        context: runtimeRecorderClone(context),\n        screen: runtimeRecorderSerializeScreen(frameDetail)\n      };\n      runtimeRecording.lastFrameAt = nowMs;\n      if (previous && runtimeRecorderFrameSignature(previous) === runtimeRecorderFrameSignature(frame)) {\n        previous.at = frame.at;\n        previous.atMs = frame.atMs;\n        previous.delayMs += frame.delayMs;\n        previous.kind = frame.kind;\n        previous.label = frame.label;\n        previous.transitionId = frame.transitionId;\n        previous.triggerEvent = frame.triggerEvent;\n        previous.eventName = frame.eventName;\n        previous.detail = frame.detail;\n        previous.screen = frame.screen;\n        syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n        return previous;\n      }\n      if (runtimeRecording.frames.length >= RUNTIME_RECORDER_MAX_FRAMES) {\n        runtimeRecording.frames.shift();\n        runtimeRecording.frames.forEach((item, index) => { item.index = index + 1; });\n      }\n      frame.index = runtimeRecording.frames.length + 1;\n      runtimeRecording.frames.push(frame);\n      runtimeRecording.index = runtimeRecording.frames.length - 1;\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n      return frame;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderQueueFrame(kind = \"snapshot\", detail = {}) {\n      if (!runtimeRecording.active || runtimeRecording.playing) return false;\n      runtimeRecording.pendingFrame = { kind, detail };\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderFlushPendingFrame() {\n      if (!runtimeRecording.pendingFrame) return null;\n      const pending = runtimeRecording.pendingFrame;\n      runtimeRecording.pendingFrame = null;\n      return runtimeRecorderCaptureFrame(pending.kind, pending.detail);\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderStopPlayback() {\n      if (runtimeRecording.timer) clearTimeout(runtimeRecording.timer);\n      runtimeRecording.timer = 0;\n      runtimeRecording.playing = false;\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderRestoreFrame(index, opts = {}) {\n      const safeIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(runtimeRecording.frames.length - 1, Number(index)));\n      const frame = runtimeRecording.frames[safeIndex];\n      if (!frame) return false;\n      if (!opts.keepTimer) runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      runtimeRecording.mode = \"replay\";\n      runtimeRecording.index = safeIndex;\n      runtimeRecording.playing = Boolean(opts.keepTimer || runtimeRecording.timer);\n      blockedTransitionId = null;\n      runtimeValidation = null;\n      runtimeRecorderRenderStoredFrame(frame);\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderPlaybackDelay(index) {\n      const value = Number(runtimeRecording.frames[index]?.delayMs);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) return RUNTIME_RECORDER_STEP_MS;\n      return Math.min(RUNTIME_RECORDER_MAX_STEP_MS, Math.max(RUNTIME_RECORDER_MIN_STEP_MS, Math.round(value)));\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderSchedulePlaybackStep() {\n      if (!runtimeRecording.playing) return;\n      let next = runtimeRecording.index + 1;\n      if (next >= runtimeRecording.frames.length) {\n        if (!runtimeRecording.loop) {\n          runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n          return;\n        }\n        next = 0;\n      }\n      runtimeRecording.timer = setTimeout(() => {\n        runtimeRecording.timer = 0;\n        if (!runtimeRecording.playing) return;\n        runtimeRecorderRestoreFrame(next, { keepTimer: true });\n        if (!runtimeRecording.loop && next === runtimeRecording.frames.length - 1) {\n          runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n          return;\n        }\n        runtimeRecorderSchedulePlaybackStep();\n      }, runtimeRecorderPlaybackDelay(next));\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderPlay() {\n      if (runtimeRecording.active || runtimeRecording.frames.length < 2) return false;\n      if (runtimeRecording.playing) {\n        runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n        return true;\n      }\n      if (runtimeRecording.index >= runtimeRecording.frames.length - 1) {\n        runtimeRecorderRestoreFrame(0);\n      } else if (runtimeRecording.mode !== \"replay\") {\n        runtimeRecorderRestoreFrame(0);\n      }\n      runtimeRecording.playing = true;\n      runtimeRecording.mode = \"replay\";\n      runtimeRecorderSchedulePlaybackStep();\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderStopControl() {\n      if (runtimeRecording.active) {\n        runtimeRecorderStop();\n        return true;\n      }\n      if (runtimeRecorderIsReplaying()) {\n        runtimeRecorderLeaveReplayMode({ renderLive: true });\n        return true;\n      }\n      runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderLeaveReplayMode(opts = {}) {\n      if (runtimeRecording.mode !== \"replay\") return;\n      runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      runtimeRecording.mode = \"live\";\n      const screen = document.getElementById(\"screen\");\n      if (screen) delete screen.dataset.replayFrame;\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n      if (opts.renderLive) render();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderStart() {\n      runtimeRecorderLeaveReplayMode({ renderLive: true });\n      runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      runtimeRecorderClearScrollTimer();\n      runtimeRecording.active = true;\n      runtimeRecording.mode = \"live\";\n      runtimeRecording.frames = [];\n      runtimeRecording.pendingFrame = null;\n      runtimeRecording.index = -1;\n      runtimeRecording.startedAt = runtimeRecorderNowMs();\n      runtimeRecording.lastFrameAt = runtimeRecording.startedAt;\n      runtimeRecording.createdAt = new Date().toISOString();\n      runtimeRecording.model = runtimeRecorderClone(model);\n      const screen = document.getElementById(\"screen\");\n      if (findState(current) && (!screen || !screen.childElementCount)) render();\n      runtimeRecorderCaptureFrame(\"start\");\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderStop() {\n      runtimeRecorderClearScrollTimer();\n      runtimeRecorderFlushPendingFrame();\n      runtimeRecording.active = false;\n      runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderResetForModel(opts = {}) {\n      runtimeRecorderStopPlayback();\n      runtimeRecorderClearScrollTimer();\n      runtimeRecording.active = false;\n      runtimeRecording.mode = \"live\";\n      runtimeRecording.pendingFrame = null;\n      if (opts.clearFrames !== false) {\n        runtimeRecording.frames = [];\n        runtimeRecording.index = -1;\n        runtimeRecording.startedAt = 0;\n        runtimeRecording.lastFrameAt = 0;\n        runtimeRecording.createdAt = \"\";\n        runtimeRecording.model = null;\n      }\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRecorderSnapshot(opts = {}) {\n      const currentFrame = runtimeRecording.frames[runtimeRecording.index] || null;\n      const includeContext = opts.context !== false;\n      return {\n        active: runtimeRecording.active,\n        mode: runtimeRecording.mode,\n        playing: runtimeRecording.playing,\n        loop: runtimeRecording.loop,\n        index: runtimeRecording.index,\n        frameCount: runtimeRecording.frames.length,\n        createdAt: runtimeRecording.createdAt || \"\",\n        model: includeContext ? runtimeRecorderClone(runtimeRecording.model || model) : null,\n        currentFrame: currentFrame\n          ? includeContext\n            ? runtimeRecorderClone(currentFrame)\n            : runtimeRecorderFrameSummary(currentFrame)\n          : null,\n        frames: runtimeRecording.frames.map(frame => includeContext ? runtimeRecorderClone(frame) : runtimeRecorderFrameSummary(frame))\n      };\n    }\n\n    function syncRuntimeRecorderControls() {\n      const recordButton = document.getElementById(\"runtimeRecordButton\");\n      const playButton = document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayPlayButton\");\n      const stopButton = document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayStopButton\");\n      const loopButton = document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayLoopButton\");\n      const exportButton = document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayExportButton\");\n      const status = document.getElementById(\"runtimeRecordStatus\");\n      const snapshot = runtimeRecorderSnapshot({ context: false });\n      const hasFrames = snapshot.frameCount > 0;\n      const replayControlsVisible = hasFrames && !snapshot.active;\n      const replayDisabled = !replayControlsVisible;\n      if (recordButton) {\n        recordButton.hidden = false;\n        recordButton.disabled = snapshot.playing || (!model.states.length && !snapshot.active);\n        recordButton.textContent = snapshot.active ? \"Stoppen\" : \"Aufnehmen\";\n        recordButton.dataset.recording = snapshot.active ? \"true\" : \"false\";\n        recordButton.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", snapshot.active ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n        recordButton.title = snapshot.active ? \"Aufnahme stoppen\" : \"Aufnahme starten\";\n      }\n      for (const button of [playButton, loopButton, exportButton]) {\n        if (!button) continue;\n        button.hidden = !replayControlsVisible;\n      }\n      if (stopButton) stopButton.hidden = !replayControlsVisible || (!snapshot.playing && snapshot.mode !== \"replay\");\n      if (playButton) {\n        playButton.disabled = replayDisabled || snapshot.frameCount < 2;\n        playButton.textContent = snapshot.playing ? \"Pause\" : \"Play\";\n        playButton.dataset.active = snapshot.playing ? \"true\" : \"false\";\n      }\n      if (stopButton) stopButton.disabled = !snapshot.playing && snapshot.mode !== \"replay\";\n      if (loopButton) {\n        loopButton.disabled = replayDisabled;\n        loopButton.dataset.active = snapshot.loop ? \"true\" : \"false\";\n        loopButton.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", snapshot.loop ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n      }\n      if (exportButton) exportButton.disabled = replayDisabled;\n      if (status) {\n        status.hidden = !hasFrames && !snapshot.active;\n        status.textContent = hasFrames\n          ? (String(Math.max(1, snapshot.index + 1)) + \"/\" + String(snapshot.frameCount))\n          : \"0/0\";\n      }\n    }\n\n    function bindRuntimeRecorderControls() {\n      const controls = document.getElementById(\"runtimeRecorderControls\");\n      if (!controls || controls.dataset.bound === \"true\") return;\n      controls.dataset.bound = \"true\";\n      document.getElementById(\"runtimeRecordButton\")?.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n        if (runtimeRecording.active) runtimeRecorderStop();\n        else runtimeRecorderStart();\n      });\n      document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayPlayButton\")?.addEventListener(\"click\", () => runtimeRecorderPlay());\n      document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayStopButton\")?.addEventListener(\"click\", () => runtimeRecorderStopControl());\n      document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayLoopButton\")?.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n        runtimeRecording.loop = !runtimeRecording.loop;\n        syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n      });\n      document.getElementById(\"runtimeReplayExportButton\")?.addEventListener(\"click\", () => runtimeRecorderDownload());\n      document.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", runtimeRecorderCapturePointerEvent, { capture: true, passive: true });\n      window.addEventListener(\"scroll\", runtimeRecorderCaptureScrollEvent, { capture: true, passive: true });\n      document.getElementById(\"screen\")?.addEventListener(\"scroll\", runtimeRecorderCaptureScrollEvent, { passive: true });\n      syncRuntimeRecorderControls();\n    }\n\n    function followTransition(transition, fromStateId, reason = \"next\") {\n      blockedTransitionId = null;\n      runtimeValidation = null;\n      const requestedTarget = findState(transition.to);\n      const runtimeTarget = resolveRuntimeTarget(transition.to, transition);\n      if (!findState(runtimeTarget)) return false;\n      applyTransitionSet(transition);\n      const requestedEntry = requestedTarget ? groupEntryState(requestedTarget.id, transition) : null;\n      log(`${fromStateId} --${transition.label || reason}--> ${runtimeTarget}`);\n      if (!enterState(runtimeTarget, transition)) return false;\n      runtimeSet(\"state.previous\", fromStateId || \"\", { source: \"flow\", eventName: \"change.state.previous\" });\n      runtimeSet(\"state.lastTransition\", transition?.id || \"\", { source: \"flow\", eventName: \"change.state.lastTransition\" });\n      runtimeSet(\"state.current\", runtimeTarget || \"\", { source: \"flow\", eventName: \"change.state.current\" });\n      if (requestedTarget && requestedEntry && requestedTarget.id !== runtimeTarget) {\n        recordRuntimeEvent(\"flow.child.entry\", {\n          type: \"flow\",\n          source: \"flow\",\n          parentId: requestedTarget.id,\n          childId: requestedEntry.id,\n          resolvedChildId: runtimeTarget,\n          transitionId: transition?.id || \"\"\n        });\n      }\n      emitRuntimeEvent(\"state.\" + eventSegment(runtimeTarget) + \".entered\", { type: \"state\", from: fromStateId || \"\", transitionId: transition?.id || \"\", source: \"flow\" });\n      runtimeRecorderQueueFrame(\"transition\", {\n        transition,\n        to: runtimeTarget,\n        triggerEvent: reason\n      });\n      if (current === runtimeTarget) runCurrentStateEntryEffects({ fetch: true });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function log(text) {\n      logLines.unshift(`[${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}] ${text}`);\n      logLines = logLines.slice(0, 6);\n    }\n\n    function safeFetchUrl(url) {\n      const raw = String(url || \"\").trim();\n      if (!raw) return \"\";\n      try {\n        const parsed = new URL(raw, window.location.href);\n        return /^https?:$/i.test(parsed.protocol) ? parsed.href : \"\";\n      } catch (_) {\n        return \"\";\n      }\n    }\n\n    function countData(data) {\n      if (Array.isArray(data)) return data.length;\n      if (data && typeof data === \"object\") return Object.keys(data).length;\n      return data === null || data === undefined || data === \"\" ? 0 : 1;\n    }\n\n    function dataSourceResult(patch) {\n      return {\n        status: \"idle\",\n        loading: false,\n        done: false,\n        ok: false,\n        data: {},\n        error: \"\",\n        statusCode: 0,\n        count: 0,\n        url: \"\",\n        ...patch\n      };\n    }\n\n    function dataSourceSignature(value) {\n      const source = normalizeDataSource(value);\n      return [source.url, source.target, source.select, source.timeoutMs, source.retries].join(\"\\u0000\");\n    }\n\n    function dataSourceRuntimeMeta(state, source) {\n      const url = renderLiteralText(source.url);\n      return {\n        stateId: state?.id || \"\",\n        entryId: stateActivationId,\n        sourceKey: dataSourceSignature({ ...source, url }),\n        url\n      };\n    }\n\n    function dataSourceResultBelongsToEntry(result, meta) {\n      return Boolean(result && meta &&\n        result.stateId === meta.stateId &&\n        result.entryId === meta.entryId &&\n        result.sourceKey === meta.sourceKey);\n    }\n\n    function dataSourceSignatureMap(m) {\n      const map = new Map();\n      for (const state of m?.states || []) {\n        const source = normalizeDataSource(state.dataSource);\n        if (!hasDataSource(source)) continue;\n        const list = map.get(source.target) || [];\n        list.push(state.id + \":\" + dataSourceSignature(source));\n        map.set(source.target, list);\n      }\n      for (const [target, list] of map) map.set(target, list.sort().join(\"|\"));\n      return map;\n    }\n\n    function changedDataSourceTargets(previousModel, nextModel) {\n      const before = dataSourceSignatureMap(previousModel);\n      const after = dataSourceSignatureMap(nextModel);\n      const targets = new Set([...before.keys(), ...after.keys()]);\n      return [...targets].filter(target => before.get(target) !== after.get(target));\n    }\n\n    function resetDataSourceContextTargets(targets) {\n      const screen = document.getElementById(\"screen\");\n      for (const target of targets) {\n        const targetPath = normalizeContextPath(target, \"\");\n        if (targetPath) {\n          runtimeSet(targetPath, dataSourceResult({ status: \"source-changed\", data: {}, count: 0, error: \"\" }), { source: \"model-sync\", silent: true, notify: false, eventName: \"change.\" + targetPath });\n        }\n      }\n      if (screen) screen.innerHTML = \"\";\n    }\n\n    function writeDataSourceResult(source, result) {\n      const target = normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\");\n      const previous = readValueAtPath(context, target);\n      const handledChange = runtimeSet(target, result, { source: \"fetch\", eventName: \"change.\" + target });\n      return { target, previous, value: result, handledChange };\n    }\n\n    function emitDataSourceEvent(state, source, write, activationId, meta) {\n      if (!dataSourceEventBelongsToCurrentState(state, source, activationId, meta)) return false;\n      const target = write?.target || normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\");\n      const value = write?.value ?? readValueAtPath(context, target);\n      const previous = write?.previous;\n      const detail = { type: \"fetch\", path: target, value, source: \"fetch\", dataSource: source, stateId: state.id };\n      if (previous !== undefined) detail.previous = previous;\n      const handledChange = Boolean(write?.handledChange);\n      const status = normalizeTransitionEvent(value?.status || (value?.ok ? \"success\" : \"done\"));\n      requestRuntimeRender();\n      const handledFetch = emitRuntimeEvent(\"fetch.\" + eventSegment(target) + \".\" + eventSegment(status), detail);\n      return handledChange || handledFetch;\n    }\n\n    // Async fetch completion is accepted only while the current bus value still\n    // carries this state-entry/source identity. No response data or promise is cached.\n    function dataSourceEventBelongsToCurrentState(state, source, activationId, meta) {\n      const target = normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\");\n      const currentResult = readValueAtPath(context, target);\n      return current === state.id &&\n        activationId === stateActivationId &&\n        dataSourceResultBelongsToEntry(currentResult, meta);\n    }\n\n    async function fetchJsonWithTimeout(url, timeoutMs) {\n      const controller = new AbortController();\n      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(url, {\n          method: \"GET\",\n          headers: { Accept: \"application/json\" },\n          cache: \"no-store\",\n          credentials: \"same-origin\",\n          signal: controller.signal\n        });\n        let json = null;\n        try {\n          json = await response.json();\n        } catch (_) {\n          throw new Error(\"Expected JSON\");\n        }\n        return { response, json };\n      } finally {\n        clearTimeout(timer);\n      }\n    }\n\n    async function runStateDataSource(state, source, activationId, meta) {\n      const requestUrl = safeFetchUrl(meta?.url || renderLiteralText(source.url));\n      if (!requestUrl) {\n        if (!dataSourceEventBelongsToCurrentState(state, source, activationId, meta)) return;\n        const write = writeDataSourceResult(source, dataSourceResult({ ...meta, status: \"error\", loading: false, done: true, error: \"Ungültige Endpunkt-URL\" }));\n        emitDataSourceEvent(state, source, write, activationId, meta);\n        return;\n      }\n      let lastError = \"\";\n      for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= source.retries; attempt += 1) {\n        try {\n          const { response, json } = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(requestUrl, source.timeoutMs);\n          if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);\n          const selected = source.select ? readValueAtPath(json, source.select) : json;\n          if (!runtimeValueIsDefined(selected)) throw new Error(\"API response contains null or undefined values\");\n          const data = selected;\n          if (!dataSourceEventBelongsToCurrentState(state, source, activationId, meta)) return;\n          const write = writeDataSourceResult(source, dataSourceResult({\n            ...meta,\n            status: \"success\",\n            loading: false,\n            done: true,\n            ok: true,\n            data,\n            statusCode: response.status,\n            count: countData(data),\n            url: requestUrl\n          }));\n          emitDataSourceEvent(state, source, write, activationId, meta);\n          return;\n        } catch (err) {\n          lastError = err?.name === \"AbortError\" ? \"Zeitüberschreitung\" : (err?.message || \"Abruf fehlgeschlagen\");\n          if (attempt < source.retries) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 200 * Math.pow(2, attempt)));\n        }\n      }\n      if (!dataSourceEventBelongsToCurrentState(state, source, activationId, meta)) return;\n      const write = writeDataSourceResult(source, dataSourceResult({\n        ...meta,\n        status: \"error\",\n        loading: false,\n        done: true,\n        error: lastError || \"Abruf fehlgeschlagen\",\n        url: requestUrl\n      }));\n      emitDataSourceEvent(state, source, write, activationId, meta);\n    }\n\n    function ensureStateDataSource(state) {\n      const source = normalizeDataSource(state.dataSource);\n      if (!hasDataSource(source)) return null;\n      const target = normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\");\n      const meta = dataSourceRuntimeMeta(state, source);\n      const currentResult = readValueAtPath(context, target);\n      if (dataSourceResultBelongsToEntry(currentResult, meta) && (currentResult.loading === true || currentResult.done === true)) {\n        return null;\n      }\n      const activationId = stateActivationId;\n      writeDataSourceResult(source, dataSourceResult({\n        ...meta,\n        status: \"loading\",\n        loading: true,\n        done: false,\n        data: {},\n        count: 0,\n        error: \"\",\n        url: meta.url\n      }));\n      return runStateDataSource(state, source, activationId, meta);\n    }\n\n    function currentDataSourceTargetChanged(targets = []) {\n      const state = stateById(current);\n      if (!state) return false;\n      const source = normalizeDataSource(state.dataSource);\n      if (!hasDataSource(source)) return false;\n      const target = normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\");\n      return targets.some(item => normalizeContextPath(item, \"\") === target);\n    }\n\n    function runCurrentStateEntryEffects({ fetch = false, defaults = true } = {}) {\n      const state = stateById(current);\n      if (!state) return;\n      if (defaults) applyActiveStateDataDefaults(state);\n      if (fetch) void ensureStateDataSource(state);\n    }\n\n    function stateFetchIsLoading(state) {\n      const source = normalizeDataSource(state?.dataSource);\n      if (!hasDataSource(source)) return false;\n      const currentResult = readValueAtPath(context, normalizeContextPath(source.target, \"fetch\"));\n      return !currentResult || currentResult.loading === true || currentResult.status === \"loading\" || currentResult.done !== true;\n    }\n\n    function refreshBlockedValidation() {\n      if (!blockedTransitionId) return;\n      const transition = model.transitions.find(t => t.id === blockedTransitionId);\n      if (!transition || conditionOk(transition.condition)) blockedTransitionId = null;\n      for (const action of document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-action-transition-id]\")) {\n        const t = model.transitions.find(item => item.id === action.dataset.actionTransitionId);\n        action.classList.toggle(\"invalid\", Boolean(t && t.id === blockedTransitionId && !conditionOk(t.condition)));\n      }\n    }\n\n    function notifyRuntime() {\n      const payload = {\n        type: \"STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_STATE\",\n        current,\n        syncId: runtimeHostModelSyncId,\n        paused: runtimeIsPaused(),\n        transitionId: transitionToNotify?.id || null,\n        from: transitionToNotify?.from || null,\n        to: current,\n        label: transitionToNotify?.label || null,\n        context: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(context))\n      };\n      postHostMessage(payload);\n      transitionToNotify = null;\n    }\n\n    const SUPPORTED_DAISY_VARIANTS = new Set([\n      \"accordion\", \"alert\", \"avatar\", \"badge\", \"bottom-navigation\", \"breadcrumbs\",\n      \"button\", \"calendar\", \"card\", \"chart\", \"carousel\", \"checkbox\", \"countdown\", \"drawer\", \"dropdown\",\n      \"feature-grid\", \"file-input\", \"footer\", \"hero\", \"indicator\", \"input\", \"loading\", \"mask\", \"menu\", \"modal\",\n      \"navbar\", \"pricing\", \"progress\", \"radial-progress\", \"figure-reverse\", \"form\", \"overlay\"]);\n\n    function runtimeDaisyVariantName(component) {\n      return String(component?.variant || \"alert\").toLowerCase();\n    }\n\n    function runtimeSupportedDaisyComponent(component) {\n      const variant = runtimeDaisyVariantName(component);\n      if (!SUPPORTED_DAISY_VARIANTS.has(variant)) return false;\n      if (variant === \"navbar\") {\n        const layout = String(daisyDataText(component, \"layout\", \"menu-submenu\")).trim() || \"menu-submenu\";\n        return SUPPORTED_DAISY_NAVBAR_LAYOUTS.has(layout);\n      }\n      if (variant === \"hero\") {\n        const layout = String(daisyDataText(component, \"layout\", \"centered\")).trim() || \"centered\";\n        return SUPPORTED_DAISY_HERO_LAYOUTS.has(layout);\n      }\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function daisyScopePath(component) {\n      return runtimeNormalizeBindingPath(component.dataPath, \"\");\n    }\n\n    function daisyScopeData(component) {\n      const value = readContextPathRaw(daisyScopePath(component));\n      return value && typeof value === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value : {};\n    }\n\n    function daisyLines(component) {\n      return renderLiteralText(component.text || \"\").split(/\\r?\\n/).map(line => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n    }\n\n    function daisyDataText(component, key, fallback = \"\") {\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      const value = data[key];\n      if (value === undefined || value === null || typeof value === \"object\") return renderLiteralText(fallback);\n      return renderLiteralText(String(value));\n    }\n\n    function daisyDataNumber(component, key, fallback = 0) {\n      const value = daisyScopeData(component)[key];\n      const number = Number(value);\n      return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : fallback;\n    }\n\n    function daisyDataBool(component, key, fallback = false) {\n      const value = daisyScopeData(component)[key];\n      return value === undefined ? fallback : Boolean(value);\n    }\n\n    function daisyDataArray(component, key, fallback = []) {\n      const value = daisyScopeData(component)[key];\n      return Array.isArray(value) ? value : fallback;\n    }\n\n    function daisyBoundPath(component, key) {\n      const scope = daisyScopePath(component);\n      return scope && key ? scope + \".\" + key : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function daisyOwnerIsCurrent(component) {\n      const ownerId = String(component?.__ownerStateId || \"\");\n      return !ownerId || ownerId === current;\n    }\n\n    function daisyOwnerCanWrite(component) {\n      return daisyOwnerIsCurrent(component);\n    }\n\n    function appendDaisyLocalActionButton(parent, component, label, onClick, className = \"\") {\n      const text = String(label || \"\").trim();\n      if (!text || !daisyOwnerCanWrite(component)) return false;\n      parent.appendChild(createDaisyButton(text, onClick, className));\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function daisyWrite(component, key, value, opts = {}) {\n      if (!daisyOwnerCanWrite(component)) return false;\n      const path = daisyBoundPath(component, key);\n      if (!path || !runtimeStateDataPathIsDeclared(path)) return false;\n      if (opts.render && opts.preserveFocus) pendingRuntimeFocusPath = path;\n      const previous = readValueAtPath(context, path);\n      const handled = runtimeSet(path, value, { source: \"daisy\", eventName: \"change.\" + path });\n      if (opts.render && !handled && previous !== readValueAtPath(context, path) && findState(current)) render();\n      return handled;\n    }\n\n    function clearDaisyCountdownTimers() {\n      for (const timer of daisyCountdownTimers.values()) clearTimeout(timer);\n      daisyCountdownTimers.clear();\n    }\n\n    function activeDaisyCountdownComponents() {\n      const state = stateById(current);\n      if (!state) return [];\n      const rows = [];\n      for (const ownerState of activeStateChain(state)) {\n        for (const component of normalizeComponents(ownerState.components)) {\n          if (component.type === \"daisy\" && String(component.variant || \"\").toLowerCase() === \"countdown\") rows.push({ ownerState, component });\n        }\n      }\n      return rows;\n    }\n\n    function resumeDaisyCountdownsAfterPause() {\n      const now = Date.now();\n      for (const { component } of activeDaisyCountdownComponents()) {\n        const scope = daisyScopePath(component);\n        if (!scope) continue;\n        const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n        if (data.finished === true || data.running === false) continue;\n        const value = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(Number(data.value)) ? Math.ceil(Number(data.value)) : 0);\n        if (value <= 0) continue;\n        writeRuntimeState(scope + \".startedAt\", now, { source: \"runtime-control\", metadata: false, token: RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN });\n        writeRuntimeState(scope + \".endsAt\", now + value * 1000, { source: \"runtime-control\", metadata: false, token: RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN });\n      }\n    }\n\n    function setRuntimePaused(paused, opts = {}) {\n      const next = Boolean(paused);\n      const previous = runtimeIsPaused();\n      writeRuntimeState(\"runtime.paused\", next, { source: \"runtime-control\", metadata: false, token: RUNTIME_WRITE_TOKEN });\n      if (next) {\n        if (continuationScheduleTimer) clearTimeout(continuationScheduleTimer);\n        continuationScheduleTimer = null;\n        continuationScheduleKey = \"\";\n        runtimeEventQueue = [];\n        clearDaisyCountdownTimers();\n      } else if (previous !== next) {\n        resumeDaisyCountdownsAfterPause();\n      }\n      if (opts.render !== false && previous !== next && findState(current)) render();\n      else if (opts.notify !== false) notifyRuntime();\n    }\n\n    function daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId) {\n      if (!ownerStateId || stateActivationId !== activationId || !findState(current)) return false;\n      return ownerStateId === current;\n    }\n\n    function daisyCountdownRemaining(component, now = Date.now()) {\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      const currentValue = Number(data.value);\n      const fallback = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(currentValue) ? Math.ceil(currentValue) : 0);\n      const endsAt = Number(data.endsAt);\n      if (runtimeIsPaused()) return fallback;\n      if (data.finished === true || data.running === false || !Number.isFinite(endsAt) || endsAt <= 0) return fallback;\n      return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((endsAt - now) / 1000));\n    }\n\n    function daisyCountdownDelay(component, now = Date.now()) {\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      if (data.finished === true || data.running === false) return null;\n      const value = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(Number(data.value)) ? Math.ceil(Number(data.value)) : 0);\n      const endsAt = Number(data.endsAt);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(endsAt) || endsAt <= 0) return value > 0 ? 0 : null;\n      const remainingMs = endsAt - now;\n      if (remainingMs <= 0) return 0;\n      const boundary = remainingMs % 1000 || 1000;\n      return Math.max(50, Math.min(1000, boundary));\n    }\n\n    function daisyCountdownSet(component, key, value) {\n      const path = daisyBoundPath(component, key);\n      if (!path) return false;\n      return runtimeSet(path, value, { source: \"daisy-countdown\", eventName: \"change.\" + path });\n    }\n\n    function tickDaisyCountdown(component, ownerStateId, activationId) {\n      if (runtimeRecorderIsReplaying()) return;\n      if (runtimeIsPaused()) return;\n      if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      if (data.finished === true || data.running === false) return;\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const value = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(Number(data.value)) ? Math.ceil(Number(data.value)) : 0);\n      let endsAt = Number(data.endsAt);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(endsAt) || endsAt <= 0) {\n        if (value <= 0) {\n          let handled = false;\n          handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"value\", 0) || handled;\n          if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n          handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"running\", false) || handled;\n          if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n          handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"finished\", true) || handled;\n          if (!handled && daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) render();\n          return;\n        }\n        daisyCountdownSet(component, \"startedAt\", now);\n        if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n        endsAt = now + value * 1000;\n        daisyCountdownSet(component, \"endsAt\", endsAt);\n        if (daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) render();\n        return;\n      }\n      const remaining = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((endsAt - now) / 1000));\n      let handled = false;\n      const valueChanged = remaining !== value;\n      if (valueChanged) {\n        handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"value\", remaining) || handled;\n        if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n      }\n      if (remaining <= 0) {\n        handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"running\", false) || handled;\n        if (!daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) return;\n        handled = daisyCountdownSet(component, \"finished\", true) || handled;\n        if (!handled && daisyCountdownOwnerIsActive(ownerStateId, activationId)) render();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (valueChanged || handled) render();\n      else scheduleDaisyCountdown(component, stateById(ownerStateId));\n    }\n\n    function scheduleDaisyCountdown(component, ownerState) {\n      if (runtimeRecorderIsReplaying()) return;\n      if (runtimeIsPaused()) return;\n      const ownerStateId = ownerState?.id || \"\";\n      if (ownerStateId !== current) return;\n      const scope = daisyScopePath(component);\n      if (!ownerStateId || !scope) return;\n      const delay = daisyCountdownDelay(component);\n      if (delay === null) return;\n      const key = ownerStateId + \":\" + scope;\n      if (daisyCountdownTimers.has(key)) return;\n      const activationId = stateActivationId;\n      const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n        daisyCountdownTimers.delete(key);\n        tickDaisyCountdown(component, ownerStateId, activationId);\n      }, delay);\n      daisyCountdownTimers.set(key, timer);\n    }\n\n    function appendDaisyText(parent, tag, className, text) {\n      const child = document.createElement(tag);\n      child.className = className;\n      child.textContent = text;\n      parent.appendChild(child);\n      return child;\n    }\n\n    function daisyClass(...parts) {\n      const tokens = [];\n      parts.flatMap(part => String(part || \"\").split(/\\s+/)).filter(Boolean).forEach(token => {\n        if (!tokens.includes(token)) tokens.push(token);\n      });\n      return tokens.join(\" \");\n    }\n\n    function createDaisyButton(label, onClick, className = \"\") {\n      const button = document.createElement(\"button\");\n      button.type = \"button\";\n      button.className = daisyClass(\"btn\", className);\n      button.textContent = label;\n      button.onclick = onClick;\n      return button;\n    }\n\n    function createDaisyChoiceButton(component, key, value, selected, className = \"\") {\n      const button = createDaisyButton(String(value), () => daisyWrite(component, key, value, { render: true }), daisyClass(\"btn-ghost daisy-choice\", className));\n      button.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", selected ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n      return button;\n    }\n\n    function daisyTransitionIdSetFromValue(value, ids, depth = 0) {\n      if (!value || depth > 6) return;\n      if (Array.isArray(value)) {\n        value.forEach(item => daisyTransitionIdSetFromValue(item, ids, depth + 1));\n        return;\n      }\n      if (typeof value !== \"object\") return;\n      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {\n        if (key === \"transitionId\") {\n          const id = String(child || \"\").trim();\n          if (id) ids.add(id);\n        } else {\n          daisyTransitionIdSetFromValue(child, ids, depth + 1);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function daisyExplicitTransitionIds(component) {\n      const ids = new Set();\n      const add = value => {\n        const id = String(value || \"\").trim();\n        if (id) ids.add(id);\n      };\n      add(component?.transitionId);\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      add(data.transitionId);\n      add(data.primaryTransitionId);\n      add(data.secondaryTransitionId);\n      for (const key of [\"items\", \"options\", \"menuItems\", \"submenu\", \"columns\", \"links\", \"plans\"]) {\n        daisyTransitionIdSetFromValue(data[key], ids);\n      }\n      return ids;\n    }\n\n    function daisyComponentTransitionId(component) {\n      const data = daisyScopeData(component);\n      return String(component?.transitionId || data.transitionId || \"\").trim();\n    }\n\n    function daisyComponentActionLabel(component, key, fallback = \"\") {\n      return {\n        label: daisyDataText(component, key, fallback),\n        transitionId: daisyComponentTransitionId(component),\n        url: daisyDataText(component, \"url\", \"\")\n      };\n    }\n\n    function daisyItemLabel(item) {\n      return item && typeof item === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(item)\n        ? String(item.label || item.title || item.name || item.value || \"\").trim()\n        : String(item || \"\").trim();\n    }\n\n    function daisyItemTransitionId(item) {\n      return item && typeof item === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(item)\n        ? String(item.transitionId || \"\").trim()\n        : \"\";\n    }\n\n    function daisyItemActionTargetConflict(item) {\n      if (!item || typeof item !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(item)) return false;\n      const rawUrl = String(item.url || item.href || \"\").trim();\n      return Boolean(daisyItemTransitionId(item) && rawUrl);\n    }\n\n    function daisyUnboundItemLabels(items) {\n      return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])\n        .filter(item => !daisyItemUrl(item) && !daisyItemTransitionId(item))\n        .map(daisyItemLabel)\n        .filter(Boolean);\n    }\n\n    function daisyItemUrl(item) {\n      if (!item || typeof item !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(item)) return \"\";\n      return safeComponentLinkUrl(String(item.url || item.href || \"\"));\n    }\n\n    function createDaisyLink(label, url, className = \"\") {\n      const href = safeComponentLinkUrl(url);\n      if (!href) return document.createTextNode(\"\");\n      const link = document.createElement(\"a\");\n      link.className = daisyClass(className || \"link link-hover\");\n      link.href = href;\n      link.textContent = label;\n      if (safeExternalComponentUrl(href)) {\n        link.target = \"_blank\";\n        link.rel = \"noopener noreferrer\";\n        link.addEventListener(\"click\", evt => {\n          if (!hasHostWindow()) return;\n          evt.preventDefault();\n          openExternalComponentLink(href);\n        });\n      }\n      return link;\n    }\n\n    function daisyTransitionMatchesComponent(transition, component) {\n      if (!component) return true;\n      return daisyExplicitTransitionIds(component).has(String(transition?.id || \"\"));\n    }\n\n    function daisyActionTransitions(ownerState, renderOptions = {}, component = null) {\n      if (!ownerState || ownerState.id !== renderOptions.activeStateId) return [];\n      const transitions = Array.isArray(renderOptions.actionTransitions) ? renderOptions.actionTransitions : [];\n      if (!component) return transitions;\n      return transitions.filter(transition => daisyTransitionMatchesComponent(transition, component));\n    }\n\n    function daisyRuntimeActionLabels(component) {\n      const variant = runtimeDaisyVariantName(component);\n      const itemsFrom = (key, fallback = []) => daisyDataArray(component, key, fallback)\n        .filter(item => !daisyItemUrl(item));\n      const itemLabels = items => items.map(daisyItemLabel).filter(Boolean);\n      if ([\"bottom-navigation\", \"drawer\", \"menu\", \"steps\", \"tabs\"].includes(variant)) return itemsFrom(\"items\");\n      if (variant === \"dropdown\") return itemsFrom(\"options\", itemLabels(itemsFrom(\"items\")));\n      if (variant === \"navbar\") {\n        const layout = daisyDataText(component, \"layout\", \"menu-submenu\");\n        if (layout === \"cart-profile\") return [{ label: daisyDataText(component, \"actionLabel\", \"Warenkorb anzeigen\"), transitionId: daisyScopeData(component).transitionId }, ...itemsFrom(\"menuItems\")].filter(item => daisyItemLabel(item));\n        if (layout === \"search-dropdown\") return itemsFrom(\"menuItems\");\n        return [...itemsFrom(\"items\"), ...itemsFrom(\"submenu\")];\n      }\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    function daisyTransitionButtonCandidates(ownerState, renderOptions = {}, component = null, options = {}) {\n      let transitions = daisyActionTransitions(ownerState, renderOptions, component);\n      const hasLabelFilter = Array.isArray(options.labels);\n      if (!hasLabelFilter) return transitions;\n      const explicitOrder = new Map();\n      options.labels.forEach((item, index) => {\n        if (daisyItemUrl(item)) return;\n        if (item && typeof item === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(item)) {\n          const id = String(item.transitionId || \"\").trim();\n          if (id && !explicitOrder.has(id)) explicitOrder.set(id, index);\n        }\n      });\n      if (!explicitOrder.size) return [];\n      return transitions\n        .filter(t => explicitOrder.has(String(t?.id || \"\")))\n        .sort((a, b) => {\n          const rank = t => {\n            const id = String(t?.id || \"\");\n            if (explicitOrder.has(id)) return explicitOrder.get(id);\n            return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;\n          };\n          return rank(a) - rank(b);\n        });\n    }\n\n    function runtimeClaimedTransitionIdsForState(state, actionTransitions) {\n      const ids = new Set();\n      const transitionIds = new Set((Array.isArray(actionTransitions) ? actionTransitions : []).map(t => String(t.id || \"\")));\n      const renderOptions = {\n        activeStateId: state?.id || \"\",\n        actionTransitions: Array.isArray(actionTransitions) ? actionTransitions : []\n      };\n      const repeat = normalizeRepeatConfig(state?.repeat);\n      for (const component of runtimeOrderedRenderComponentsForState(state, repeat)) {\n        if (component.type === \"transitionButton\") {\n          const id = String(component.transitionId || \"\");\n          if (transitionIds.has(id)) ids.add(id);\n          continue;\n        }\n        if (component.type !== \"daisy\" || !runtimeSupportedDaisyComponent(component)) continue;\n        for (const id of daisyExplicitTransitionIds(component)) {\n          if (transitionIds.has(id)) ids.add(id);\n        }\n        const labels = daisyRuntimeActionLabels(component);\n        const transitions = labels\n          ? daisyTransitionButtonCandidates(state, renderOptions, component, { labels })\n          : daisyActionTransitions(state, renderOptions, component);\n        transitions.forEach(transition => ids.add(transition.id));\n      }\n      return ids;\n    }\n\n    function appendDaisyTransitionButtons(parent, ownerState, renderOptions, source, options = {}) {\n      const transitions = daisyTransitionButtonCandidates(ownerState, renderOptions, options.component, options);\n      if (Array.isArray(options.labels)) {\n        const byId = new Map(transitions.map(t => [String(t.id || \"\"), t]));\n        let rendered = 0;\n        options.labels.forEach(item => {\n          if (daisyItemActionTargetConflict(item)) return;\n          const url = daisyItemUrl(item);\n          const label = daisyItemLabel(item);\n          if (url) {\n            const link = createDaisyLink(label || url, url, options.buttonClass || \"btn-ghost daisy-choice\");\n            if (options.listItem) {\n              const listItem = document.createElement(\"li\");\n              listItem.appendChild(link);\n              parent.appendChild(listItem);\n            } else {\n              parent.appendChild(link);\n            }\n            rendered += 1;\n            return;\n          }\n          const explicitId = daisyItemTransitionId(item);\n          const transition = explicitId ? byId.get(explicitId) : null;\n          if (!transition) return;\n          const button = createDaisyButton(label || runtimeTransitionLabel(transition), () => {\n            if (Date.now() < suppressActionUntil) return;\n            emitRuntimeEvent(transitionEventName(transition), { type: \"button\", source, transitionId: transition.id });\n          }, options.buttonClass || \"btn-primary daisy-transition-button\");\n          if (!button.classList.contains(\"daisy-transition-button\")) button.classList.add(\"daisy-transition-button\");\n          if (options.role) button.setAttribute(\"role\", options.role);\n          if (options.activeClass && String(runtimeTransitionLabel(transition)) === String(options.activeValue ?? \"\")) button.classList.add(options.activeClass);\n          button.dataset.transitionId = transition.id;\n          if (transition.id === renderOptions.defaultActionId) button.dataset.defaultAction = \"true\";\n          applyRuntimeTransitionButtonStyle(button, transition);\n          if (options.listItem) {\n            const listItem = document.createElement(\"li\");\n            listItem.appendChild(button);\n            parent.appendChild(listItem);\n          } else {\n            parent.appendChild(button);\n          }\n          rendered += 1;\n        });\n        return rendered;\n      }\n      transitions.forEach(t => {\n        const button = createDaisyButton(runtimeTransitionLabel(t), () => {\n          if (Date.now() < suppressActionUntil) return;\n          emitRuntimeEvent(transitionEventName(t), { type: \"button\", source, transitionId: t.id });\n        }, options.buttonClass || \"btn-primary daisy-transition-button\");\n        if (!button.classList.contains(\"daisy-transition-button\")) button.classList.add(\"daisy-transition-button\");\n        if (options.role) button.setAttribute(\"role\", options.role);\n        if (options.activeClass && String(runtimeTransitionLabel(t)) === String(options.activeValue ?? \"\")) button.classList.add(options.activeClass);\n        button.dataset.transitionId = t.id;\n        if (t.id === renderOptions.defaultActionId) button.dataset.defaultAction = \"true\";\n        applyRuntimeTransitionButtonStyle(button, t);\n        if (options.listItem) {\n          const item = document.createElement(\"li\");\n          item.appendChild(button);\n          parent.appendChild(item);\n        } else {\n          parent.appendChild(button);\n        }\n      });\n      return transitions.length;\n    }\n\n    function createDaisyComponentElement(component, ownerState = null, renderOptions = {}) {\n      if (ownerState?.id) component = { ...component, __ownerStateId: ownerState.id };\n      const variant = runtimeDaisyVariantName(component);\n      if (!runtimeSupportedDaisyComponent(component)) return null;\n      const lines = daisyLines(component);\n      const title = daisyDataText(component,   if (item && typeof item === \"object\") return String(item.label || item.title || item.name || item.value || \"Eintrag\");\n        return String(item);\n      }).filter(Boolean);\n      const parent => parent.appendChild(appendDaisyText(document.createElement(\"span\"), \"a\", \"btn btn-ghost text-xl\", brand));\n        const makeMenu = (className = \"menu menu-horizontal px-1\") => {\n       const trigger = document.createElement(\"div\");\n          trigger.tabIndex = 0;\n          trigger.setAttribute(\"role\", \"button\");\n          trigger.onclick = () => daisyWrite(component,     };\n        const avatars = daisyDataArray(component, \"avatars\", daisyDataArray(component, \"items\", []));\n        if (avatars.length) {\n          const group =     }\n      }\n      return rendered;\n    }\n\n\n    function runtimeDataLabel(path, fallback = \"Value\") {\n      const parts = String(path || \"\").split(\".\").filter(Boolean).filter(part =>    }\n\n    function runtimeColumnarRepeatEntries(value) {\n      if (!value || typeof value !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(value)) return [];\n      const groups = new Map();\n      Object.entries(value).forEach(([key, child]) => {\n        if (!Array.isArray(child) || !child.length) return;\n        if (!child.some(runtimeIsScalarDataValue)) return;\n        const group = groups.get(child.length) || [];\n        group.push({ key, value: child });\n        groups.set(child.length, group);\n      });\n      return [...groups.values()]\n        .filter(group => group.length >= 2)\n        .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length || (b[0]?.value.length || 0) - (a[0]?.value.length || 0))[0] || [];\n    }\n\n    function runtimeColumnarRepeatItem(value, index = 0) {\n      const item = {};\n      runtimeColumnarRepeatEntries(value).forEach(entry => { item[entry.key] = entry.value[index]; });\n      return item;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeRepeatValueItems(value) {\n      if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;\n      const entries = runtimeColumnarRepeatEntries(value);\n      if (entries.length < 2) return null;\n      return      const raw = String(url || \"\").trim();\n      if (!raw || /^https?:\\/\\/?$/i.test(raw)) return \"\";\n      if (/^https?:\\/\\//i.test(raw)) return raw;\n      if (/^data:image\\/(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg\\+xml);base64,/i.test(raw)) return raw;\n      if (/^(?:\\/assets\\/|\\.\\/assets\\/|assets\\/)[^\\s<>\"']*$/i.test(raw)) {\n        try {\n          const base = location.protocol === \"blob:\" && location.origin && location.origin !== \"null\"\n            ? location.origin + \"/\"\n            : document.baseURI;\n          return new URL(raw, base).href;\n        } catch (_) {\n          return raw;\n        }\n      }\n      return \"\";\n    }\n\n    function safeComponentLinkUrl(url) {\n      const raw = String(url || \"\").trim();\n      if (!raw || /[\\s<>\"']/.test(raw)) return \"\";\n      if (/^https?:\\/\\//i.test(raw)) return raw;\n      if (/^(?:mailto:|tel:)[^\\s<>\"']+$/i.test(raw)) return raw;\n      if (/^(?:\\.\\/|\\/)?(?:state\\.html|index\\.html)(?:[?#][^\\s<>\"']*)?$/i.test(raw)) {\n        if (location.protocol !== \"blob:\" || !location.origin || location.origin === \"null\") return raw;\n        try { return new URL(raw, location.origin + \"/\").href; } catch (_) { return raw; }\n      }\n      if (/^#[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(raw)) return raw;\n      return \"\";\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionSource(t);\n      const storedHue = Number(source?._runtimeHue ?? t?._runtimeHue);\n      if (Number.isFinite(storedHue)) return storedHue;\n      const layerId = runtimeTransitionLineLayerId(t);\n      const index = Math.max(0, runtimeTransitionsInLayer(layerId).findIndex(item => item.id === source?.id));\n      return (195 + index * 137.508) % 360;\n    }\n\n    function runtimeTransitionColor(t) {\n      const hue = runtimeTransitionHue(t).toFixed(1);\n      return `hsl(${hue} 84% 62%)`;\n    }\n\n    function applyRuntimeTransitionButtonStyle(button, t) continuationScheduleKey = \"\";\n        return;\n      }\n      const eligibleTransitions = transitions.filter(t => conditionOk(t.condition));\n      const transition = eligibleTransitions.find(t =>
